the user's word of 2026-11-09, a home page made with 1.0.0's cards keeping their plain look after 1.1.0 brought the cards' design): as the workspace's server starts (`adoptWorkspace`, the release's `startProductionServer`), whenever its catalog reads the workspace's blocks (`readCatalog`, by the hook `followOnRead` sets for the default home: all documents use those blocks, project-local copies are not read, and a command sets no hook) and when the page service reads them (its library, an add), each is brought up to Gallery's as `add` brings it, the manifest written last, once a process for each block and version, the server's log saying it once, a line for all; one changed since it was added stays as it is, the log saying so once, and 设置 › 组件列表's row (`newer`, `shippedNewer`) and `list` saying that Gallery has a newer version and how to take it (delete the folder, then `gallery block add <name>`), what a system writes into a folder it shows (`.DS_Store`, `._*`, `Thumbs.db`, `desktop.ini`: `SYSTEM_FILE`) being in no fingerprint, taken out by no update and copied by no recipe; `new <id> --recipe <name>` writes a block of one's own of one of them (`recipeFiles`: every word naming it, its id and the id with `-` for `/`, naming the new one in one pass, so that a new id holding the old one, `local/gallery-card` of `gallery/card`, is named once, its version 0.1.0), and `new` writes any block's files with its manifest last whatever order they come in (`writingOrder`), so that a block a running server sees is whole and one a failed write left is a folder with no manifest; a static block with no script unless `--script` asks for one, and with `--flow` a slot, `body`, in place of its text paragraph (a paragraph and a list in the example's, an empty paragraph in the template's; no class on it, as on the instance, a name of a custom block being free to start with `doc-`), with `--data` a block that keeps what a reader enters (`dataScript`: a part `items`, a script of JSON written on one line, as `context.editJSON` writes a part that holds one, which `--script` is implied by; a `kit.js` reading it with `context.readJSON` and writing it with `context.editJSON`, from a `DocKit.input` field and a button for each line, each drawn hidden by the `hidden` attribute where `context.canEdit()` is false; an `example()` that adds a line by the function the field calls, so that it works on `template.html`'s empty instance, which the check runs it on too) and with `--page` a custom page's card (`places` `["page"]`, `card.sizes` `[[4, 3], [2, 3]]` with `resize`, its text parts edited as `none`, which a page shows as written), each alone or both (`--flow` with either is refused: a page has no slot, and the data part is what the slot would be), each passing `check`, `try` on its field (`--type .component-field`) and, as a card, `gallery page try`, as it stands, and `--recipe`'s last line saying what is still the recipe's (writing.md and `example.html`; a recipe copies none of its pictures, `recipeFiles`, the new block's being its first passing check's); `list` and `show` are the live answers to what there is and how to write it, nothing of them being written into the skill: `list` in two groups by `places` (文档里可用, 自定义页可用, a block of both said whole in the first), a block the reader's switch keeps out of the document's menus still one to write with, then what does not run and why, then Gallery's own blocks the workspace has not added or has as an older version, each with its `gallery block add` line, or as a copy changed since it was added that Gallery has a newer version of (`versionAfter`), with how to take Gallery's. `show` and `check` take a name with no scope that no block of `local` has and one of Gallery's own has as that one's (`blockNamed`: `gallery block show heatmap` shows `gallery/heatmap`, as `add` and `page add` take the bare name; the blind test of 2026-11-09 had it refused, "No block local/heatmap"), a block of the workspace's own by that name staying `local`'s, and `local/<name>` written where Gallery has a block of that name is refused with the hint `Gallery has a block of that name, gallery/<name>`. `show` of one of Gallery's own the workspace has not added (`showShipped`) prints what Gallery ships of it the same way, its parts, writing.md and example, and ends with the `gallery block add` line (for a block of a page, saying that `gallery page add` puts a card of it on a page); `check` and `try` of it still say how it gets in. Section 1 of `check` adds what a manifest alone cannot say: a note on each text part of a block whose places hold `page` (read-only on a custom page), a note on a `card` of one whose places hold none, and a failure where a block with `settings` has a template whose settings part is no `<script type="application/json">` holding an object. Section 4 fails a template whose slot holds what "/" cannot make there (`unmadeInSlots`, read in the file as written, at its line): the menus make a template's slot as a paste of it makes blocks (`blocksOfTemplate` in `installed/page.mjs`, the HTML importer of `clipboard/import/`), which makes a table of its cells and never a chart (`data-doc-chart`), a card (`<figure>`), a table's `<caption>` or an instance of a block, so a new block from "/" would hold something else than the template says; the first `gallery/chart-card`, a chart table in its slot, came out of "/" a plain table. `check` holds a custom block to more than `gallery check` holds a document that uses it, a custom block the user turned off as any (`readCatalog`'s `on`, a note saying so), the setting being theirs and nothing its writer can mend. `template.html` is required there, as what a menu can put in (`templateProblem`), and a text part holds words and marks alone: what the scan would lock is said. `words` gets a note where the name is Chinese and no word of it reads as pinyin of as many syllables as the name has characters, the fewest it can be read as (`fewestSyllables()`, as `fenbiao` reads as `fen-bi-a-o` too): "/" goes by the letters an input method is composing, and finds the block while its whole name is composed only by a word holding them all; where the name has no Chinese and the words have some, by a word of pinyin at all. A file that is not UTF-8 is said as that and read no further. `kit.js` is read by `diagnoseScript` (`typecheck.mjs`), one program of the SDK and the script as the function of its own it runs in: a syntax error, what a browser throws on and what the SDK does not say fail, and a type's word on what a browser runs as written is a note, as in a document's script. The names `katex` and `renderMathInElement`, which no document's script has, are there for a custom block whose `assets` ask for them. A script in which no `DocKit.component` can be read is a note, what runs being the judge. Every selector of `style.css` has its subject in the instance (`component-style.mjs`), which is why a rule there starts at the instance's own attribute and never at a class; in nesting, `&` is what the rule around it stands for, so that a neighbour is beside the instance only when that is the instance, and a selector whose `&` stands only in a function, which CSS reads with nothing in front, reaches the page. Of a custom block with a slot, a selector that stands in the instance and goes down from it by a blank (one nested with no combinator of its own, or in an @scope of the instance, goes down so) is a note, once at the rule that goes down (`slotReach`): it styles the slot's blocks too, which read as the page's, and a step of `>` or an @scope whose lower edge is a slot (`to ([data-doc-slot])`) stops short of them. `template.html` and `example.html` go through `checkReports`, each as a page written on one line before its text, so that a line of the page is a line of the file, and a failure's `line N` is the problem's line. The last section (`component-look.mjs`) runs the example and the template in a temporary document of a workspace of its own, exported by `offlineReports`, in the browser of `gallery qa`: its console and policy, the dialogs and windows it opens, a page that loads itself again, each put-back of an instance and what piles up in it or anywhere else on the page (said once for an instance, of its outermost kind, what it holds piling with it, and with the `runtime.generated` entry to write: what dehydrate then leaves and what an example then changes outside its parts at that instance are the same fault and go unsaid, `seenSaid`), a slot the component wrote in or moved (`slotsKept`: where each slot stands among the instance's tags and the tags of the blocks it holds, as an export of the view would keep them, the file's against what the page first shows, then that and its markup against each put-back and against its example's edit, each a failure that says when; what the kit draws in a slot, a formula, the spacing, a chart, is no component's, so every other comparison of what an instance shows empties its slots first, `bare`), a text part it redrew, a data part a mount changed (what a page compares it with is the file's, to the byte), the way back to the file, a page wider than the window at each width, and the pictures, in `.gallery/qa/components/<id>/`, the three widths of each theme side by side first (`overview-light.png`, `overview-dark.png`, `sideBySide()`: made in the tab once the look is done, from the six pictures as data: addresses; every run of the skill test of 2026-11-05 opened two of six at most), then the page as the editing page draws it at 1024 and 360px, light (`edit-1024-light.png`, `edit-360-light.png`: the export opened again, told `doc-editable` with no one to take an edit, so that `context.canEdit()` is true and every edit refused; what a component draws only where a reader can edit, a handle or a button, which an export leaves out; the line says when they are byte for byte the export's pictures at their width, a component that draws nothing more there, as the board, whose cursor alone follows `canEdit()`), of the example above a line and the template below it, each under its file's name on its first line (`lookedAtMarkup()`: one after the other with nothing between, they read as one block twice, the skill test of 2026-11-02). The way back is what a custom block offers of it, `example(host, context)` of its registration, a small edit as a reader's gesture would make it, tried once the page has said `doc-editable`, the page standing in for the editor as `DocKit.example` runs it, refusing too what the editor refuses that the server never sees (a part's own tag or attributes changed, the part on the page written instead of its draft): it edits a data part and changes nothing else, `FILES.installed.rewrite` takes what it wrote (`partsWritten`, `source.mjs`, the same code a commit goes through), and with the part put back as an undo does the instance shows what it showed. What it wrote is told on a `way back` line of its own, neither a problem nor a note: each part with the lines of it the write takes out and puts in, as a diff counts them (`changedRuns()`, of the part as the file holds it and as the server would write it), of its lines that hold more than white space, as are all its lines it is said to have (`linesChanged()`: a script's JSON and a body's rows start and end on lines of white space alone), and whether the server took it and the drawing came back. A write that lays its part out anew is a note (`laidAnew()`): what a run of changed lines keeps at its start or its end, white space aside, standing on another number of lines (`1 line became 5`: JSON written back in a layout of its own), or a line of white space alone left (a row moved without the blank before it). An example is to call the change a reader's gesture calls (skills/gallery/references/block-script.md, "Writing back"), so that these say what a drag or a press writes; the skill test of 2026-11-05 found a JSON's first press laying 11 lines out as 17 and a drag leaving a blank, both passed with a bare `ok`. Once an instance has heard it can write, the browser's DevTools say what its elements listen to (`dropsRefused()`: `DOMDebugger.getEventListeners`, the scripts known by their address through `Debugger`, its pauses skipped): one with a `dragover` listener of `kit.js` and no `dragenter` listener on itself or on what holds it up to the instance, nor one of the document's or the window's that cancels a `dragenter` fired at it (`enterCancelled()`: such a listener hears every drag of the page, whoever's, so the browser is asked what it does), is a note at the line of `kit.js` that listens, since a drop goes where both were cancelled and the move that enters a new target asks `dragenter` alone, so that a release just then drops nothing. A hook of the kit's is what makes it the component's own gesture and no guess of the check's at what an edit of an unknown part could be. With `--documents` (`lookAtDocuments()`), the instances of the custom block the workspace's documents hold go through the same look, a section of their own for each document holding some (`documentsHolding()`: its file read as written, each instance in no other of the custom block, wherever else it stands, one in another custom block's slot taken out of it alone; one in another of its own goes in that one's markup), (home's documents, and those of the workspace `gallery open` finds when it is another, named by their file: `gallery.mjs` hands it on as `documents`), in a temporary document of a workspace of their own, each under a line naming its place (`<slug>/doc.html:<line>`), with a copy of the document's `assets/` and of each custom block the markup names, exported and opened in one browser for them all (`exportedDocument()`, `runPage()`), each finding at the instance's line of the document's file, the pictures in `documents/<slug>/` of the custom block's folder: an instance an Agent or a reader filled as the example never is (a long title, an empty table, a script of data a newer version of it wrote) is tried as the reader's page runs it, and no document is written. A look runs the instances that stand in no instance, one by one (`outerInstances()`, which `instancesIn()`, `putBack()`'s `hosts` and `wayBackSaid()` all number them by, so that the way back of one is said of that one: the review of 2026-11-07 found the way back of a page's third instance measured against one in the first's slot). Before it, the instance hears `editable` and is put back once, as a page opening on it mounts it then; an edit at any moment but in `example`, as it hears that, as it mounts, or as it hears `source` after, is one with no gesture, which a page would write each time it opened, and fails, each moment once (`unasked`): it is not taken, and one that changes nothing is, as the editor takes it. One with a data part and no `example` has a note, nothing having tried that way, but where its one data part is its `settings` (`SETTINGS_PART`, `boards` of `putBack()`), which the board's form writes and the block only reads (`gallery/countdown`). The workspace holds a copy of what reaches a page, each file written anew, so that a custom block no one can write to is checked as any; its copy of `kit.js` ends in a `sourceURL`, so that a stack names lines of `kit.js`, which `packageScript` keeps. What the page opens with is `loading`, a minute, and nothing shorter decides it, since a script that never ends keeps the load from coming; whatever stops the look is said as the section's and the check ends with its verdict, and a folder it cannot write is `unverified`.

**The menus' pictures of a block** (the owner, 2026-11-17: "/" and 添加自定义块 show beside a row the block's real, whole picture, not a drawing of it, made as the block is made, light and dark; [editor.md](editor.md) "Its picture in \"/\" and in 添加自定义块" for the card). `check` makes them as a side of its look, in the same browser once the look ran through with no problem (`lookAtPackage`'s `picture`, `src/cli/block-picture.mjs` `blockPictures`), and writes them into the block's folder as `preview-light.webp` and `preview-dark.webp` (`PICTURES`) only once the whole check passes, each only where its bytes are not what is there (a server's catalog hears no change of a block checked again unchanged), saying nothing of them: nothing for an Agent to run or to look at, its burden only going down (it drew a `preview.svg` before, which nothing reads now). Not of Gallery's own blocks (`picturesWritten`: their packs ship theirs, and a copy as added stays as Gallery ships it, for `followShipped`), nor of one a pack installed (`.gallery/blocks.json`: its pack brought its own, and its folder stays as installed); a recipe copies none (`recipeFiles`). What is pictured is the first instance of `example.html`, where the block lives. A card (its places hold `page`) stands alone on a board in a custom page of its own, in its card's frame at the size a new card takes (`defaultSize`; one the page measures, as tall as its content), the page's ground around it: the custom page's tags, the kit's and the board's, each a copy of its file in one file (`offlineCustomPage` in `pipeline.mjs`), opened in a context of its own where nothing reaches the network but what answers it here: a read of the workspace (`workspace.query`, its fetch of `QUERY_ROUTE`, under the drive on Windows as a file's page names it) is answered by the workspace's facts as the server answers it (`createIndex(...).query`, the documents as their pages show them, the record of edits), a fetch of the net (`doc-net-fetch`) by the server's own route (`createNetRoute(...).answer`: the hosts the reader allowed that block, the workspace's keys and cache; one not allowed is refused for its consent, the card then showing its 允许访问 bar, as on the reader's page), a document's icon (`/__gallery/edit/icon`) by `iconImage`. A block of documents is its whole example in a document's page as its export draws it (a view of a table reads the table above it; what it reads of the workspace is what an export embeds of it: `snapshotOf`, the workspace's facts as a card's reads are answered, and its own document's, the picture's page, when it was made and last written and its words, so that a block reading them, a line under a title of when the document was made, is pictured as it reads and not with its offline words, 2026-11-18; the tables an export keeps are those its instances asked the server for, none here), cut to its first instance, in a reading column of `READING` (448: `WIDEST` less the ground around it, the kit's `--doc-content-max-width` outweighed, the window a desktop's 800), so that its picture is shown at its own size and its words read as in a document: the block as it stands in a column that wide (a column of 分栏), laid out again, not the 712 of the reading width scaled to little over half (a 看板 shown so had words of 6px, 2026-11-18); one whose content takes more than that (`overflowOf`: a 看板's three columns at their least) stands in a column as wide as it takes, whole, and is scaled down. Each theme in turn (`prefers-color-scheme`), once what it asked for was answered and the page holds still (`settle`), is cut to the card or the instance with `MARGIN` (16) of the ground around it (`clipOf`), at `DENSITY` (2) device pixels to a CSS pixel, a picture wider than `WIDEST` (480) scaled down to it, one taller than `TALLEST` (900) cut there, as WebP at quality 90: some 3 to 30 KB each. The language is the one Gallery writes in (`fileLanguage`). Gallery's own two packs have theirs made by a maintainer, `node src/cli/pack-pictures.mjs [<id>…]`, the same way in a workspace made for them (a few documents with their words and to-dos, a record of edits over the past months), once in Chinese (their examples') and once in English (`src/cli/pack-pictures-english.mjs`: each example as an English writer fills it, and the documents' titles and to-dos), the English pair named by `LANGUAGE_PICTURES`. So that a picture reads the same whenever it is made, the page's clock stands at 10:30 of the day of the run (`clock`: a morning's greeting and sky, not the hour a maintainer ran it), the workspace's facts at that time too, a habit's dates are moved up to that day (`keptUpTo`) and the weather is a forecast of the script's (`forecastOf`, the cards' `net`): nothing reaches the net, and a run again the same day writes the same bytes. They are kept in git under `src/packs/` and looked at before they ship, not made by the release's build (a build stays the same on any machine and offline), with `src/cli/pack-pictures.json`, what they were made of (`madeFrom`: a hash of the kit's files and the scripts that make them, and one of each block's own files but its pictures and its writing notes): `tests/unit/block-pictures.mjs` fails, naming the blocks and the command, once that code has changed (a change of the kit's, every picture), so that a block's look and its picture never part; run it then (some 80 seconds for both packs), look at the pictures and commit them with the record. A copy of a block added to a workspace takes them as it takes its files (`followShipped` follows its fingerprint, not its version). A picture's size in its WebP header is read by `webpSize`.

**Gallery's own view of a table** (`src/packs/gallery/metric`): a custom block whose places are a document's and whose needs hold `tables`, reading the table its `data-doc-from` names by `context.table()` and its settings from its data part `settings` (JSON, written by its own ⚙ at its head where the page edits, `context.editJSON`, one undo step: a document has no card form), in "/" like any block ([editor.md](editor.md) "Table views": one made there is bound to the nearest table above it, and its handle's 设置… picks another table and changes the settings the manifest lists, so does its own ⚙). **指标** (`gallery/metric`) is the rows counted (`op` "count", the default), or the `sum` or `average` of a column's numbers (`column`, else the first column of numbers; a cell with no number left out), over every row or those whose cell of `where` is `is`, named by `title` or by what it counts, `unit` after the number (both typed in where they stand, `DocKit.text`, each its setting, emptied taken out; 0.3.1), formatted by `Intl.NumberFormat` (two decimals at most); a line under it only where there is more to know (a count of one value: the rows in all; a sum or an average: the rows with no number left out, 0.3.1). It draws in an export as in the page, read-only, its words too (scenario `shippedViewsExport`; typed in where they stand in the page: `shippedViewsWords`, `shippedViewsWordsLook`; Ctrl+Z in them: `shippedViewsWordsKept`), and typing in a table of 500 rows it reads stays under the bound of `tableViewLatency` (scenario `shippedViewsLatency`: its share of the main thread's CPU time a keystroke against the same table with no view, in the same run, at most 60% on the median and at most 3 times the no-view median at the 90th percentile). The pack held a board (`gallery/kanban`) and a chart (`gallery/chart-view`) over a table too until its 1.2.0 (2026-11-23), whose place the 多维表格's views took ("Databases" above): a workspace's start takes them out where they are as installed (`installPack`), and a document that still names one shows it as a block that is not there, its table as ever. `gallery check` notes a view written above the table it reads (`tableViews` in `src/render/chart-declarations.mjs`: it works there, the block's writing.md and example write the table first; a note, not a failure, naming the instance and the table's key).

**Trying one.** `gallery block try <slug> [<instance id>] <gesture> [--then <key>] [--keep]`, or `--steps <file>` in place of the gesture (`src/cli/component-try.mjs`; alone of the block commands it runs in the workspace `gallery open` finds, `--workspace`, `GALLERY_WORKSPACE`, the nearest `gallery.json`, else home, where its document is, while the block and the writes the server must have told of are home's, `readCatalog(home)`; the trial itself, `trial` with its read back, takes where it is made as its host, `documentHost` here and a custom page's card for `gallery page try`: "A custom page"), makes what a reader does on an instance of a document, the gestures its component answers, which the check's `example` stands in for and never makes: of the two runs of the retest of 2026-11-05 one drove its own Chrome by the DevTools protocol, a hundred lines of its own, and the other skipped the step as too long, asking for this command. A gesture is `--press <selector>`, `--drag <selector> --to <selector>`, `--key <selector> <key>`, `--type <selector> <words>` or `--select <selector> <value>` (`stepOf()`; `pairsOf()` of `component.mjs` takes the second argument of `--key`, `--type` and `--select` out of the command line, so that the selector is the option's value as any's), and `--then <key>` a key pressed after it where the focus is (a field that writes on Enter); `--steps` names a JSON list of them, each an object naming one gesture as the options do without their `--` (`{ "type": [".name", "多喝水"], "then": "Enter" }`, `{ "drag": ".card", "to": ".column:nth-child(3)" }`), twenty at most (`stepsOf()`: a file that is not there or does not read, and a step that is none, are mistakes in the command line), made one after another in one page (since 2026-11-07: a single gesture could not try a rename, typing then Enter, nor any form). The instance is named by its `data-block-id`, or by its custom block's id where the document holds one of it, and not at all where it holds one (`chosenInstance()`; a block an address names is that id); it is the instance numbered so among those `doc.html` holds, one in another's slot too, said with where it stands (`instancesIn()`, `placeSaid()`: `in the slot body of local/brief at doc.html:15`), as the page finds them (`hostAt()`, every instance in the page's order). By default the trial is made on a scratch copy of the document (`scratchCopy()`: its `doc.html` as the file holds it, its `doc.json` and `assets/`, in `.gallery/try/try-<8 hex>/`, a slug no document has, which `documentDirectory` of `shared/workspace.mjs` serves as any document's where no folder at the root has its name, and which no list, search or watcher sees: the server's watcher leaves `.gallery/` alone but its pages), so that `doc.html` is never written and no page of the user's shows the trial, whatever comes of it; the copy, and what the edit state keeps of it by its slug (`.gallery/state/<kind>/<slug>/`: its recent versions, the answers to its commits, its review), go at its end (`dropScratch()`), never left to an undo, and a copy a trial killed midway left is taken away by the next trial an hour on (`dropStale()`). `--keep` makes it on the document itself and leaves what it wrote, as the command did before the copy. The workspace's server is found or started as `gallery open` finds it (`serverFor`, of the document itself), and the copy's page alone (`agentPageOf`, the Agent's own address, `?agent`: its saves no reader's edit, the Agent's in the recent versions and the record of edits) opens in the browser `gallery qa` starts (`startBrowser`, `lookingArguments`), 1024px wide, light, as tall as the instance needs and with it in view (`DocKit.reveal` first), once it has come to edit (`[data-doc-ready]`, `data-ge-editing`, `doc-editable` heard and `data-ge-save` saved) and the instance holds still. The first gesture waits until the server has told the page of each write the disk holds (`toldBy()`: the newest write of `gallery.json` and what stands under `components/`, and of `doc.html` with `--keep`, then the catalog's first look at a change, `FIRST_LOOK` of `catalog-watch.mjs`, the stillness a write is told after, settle's `QUIET`, and half a second for a page told to load itself again), which costs nothing when nothing was written in the last two seconds; a page that loads itself again before it is taken again as it comes to edit, three times at most: a page opened a moment after its custom block changed hears of that from its server a moment later (the release smoke test's first run of the command, the custom block copied in just before, lost its page so). An instance in its fallback (`data-doc-fallback`) on a page asked for before the server took its custom block as the disk holds it (`takenBy()`, by the catalog's rule: `FIRST_LOOK` and `QUIET` after the newest write of the custom block's folder or of `gallery.json`, another custom block's writing waiting for nothing; or, while the disk reads the custom block as not loading and the server's catalog in use, `GET /__gallery/workspace/components`, keeps it as it was, `HOLD` after that write) is waited for until the page loads itself again with it, five seconds past the take at most (`untilTaken()`): the release smoke test's run of the command on the scorecard, copied in with its document written just before, judged its component failed by the fallback the page showed until the server took the custom block, which the page loaded itself again for a moment later. A write is known there by its file's time, which a file copied in keeps from where it was (`fs.copyFileSync`, `cp -p`, `Copy-Item`), and the server goes by what the files hold: so the take is also `FIRST_LOOK` and `QUIET` past a look that found what the disk holds of the custom block changed since the one before (its stamp, as the catalog stamps it, whatever the times), and past the first look that found the server behind the disk, the disk reading the custom block as loading where the catalog in use has it otherwise (a manifest mended by a copy, which the server had as not loading, and took a moment after the command had given up on it). A wait that is to last more than a moment, or has, is said on a `Waiting:` line, what it waits for and how long at most, and how it ended on a `Waited:` line: the page loaded itself again, or the wait ran to its end with the server keeping the custom block as it was still, behind the disk still, or the page not loading again. A custom block `gallery.json` turns off is not waited for, and one it turns off before the command starts is said so at once, exit 1 (`readCatalog` stands it with those that do not load, `off`). Each word the page says is kept with the page it was heard on (`listen()` counts the tab's navigations), and a page taken again forgets what the pages before it said, keeping what it said itself (`takenAgain()`): a mount that threw on the page before a mended custom block's is no failure of the mended one. An instance that stays in its fallback is a failure, said in the console's words of its custom block, each at its line of `kit.js` (a mount that threw), a script that does not parse by its SyntaxError alone, at the line the browser gives (what a page throws and nothing catches is heard by the DevTools protocol, `Runtime.exceptionThrown`, which says where: a parse error has no stack, as none of the script ran, and the kit's words on a script that started no component are left out), else by what the server holds of it (`fallbackSaid()`: turned off, behind the disk, not loading and why, no such custom block, or a page served before the take that did not load again). Each selector is the first element of the instance it finds, which must be shown (a box on the page), else it is a mistake in the command line, said with the classes the instance shows; but where the console has named the custom block meanwhile, or a dialog opened, the component did not draw it: a failure, with what the console said. A press is one at its element's middle, said when something else stands there (`coveredBy()`); a drag is pressed at the middle of the one and moved by `Input.dispatchMouseEvent` (puppeteer's mouse) in steps, a nudge past what starts a drag and ten to just short of where the line between the two middles comes onto the other's box, then onto its middle, released a tenth of a second later (`dragPath()`): the move before the release is the one that comes onto the target, so that a list that cancels `dragover` and not `dragenter`, which drops nothing then (`dropsRefused()`, the check's note), drops nothing here too, as a reader's release on a card's words; a drag that ends on what it drags (`--to` finding the same element, or one in it: `endsOnItself()`), or whose two middles stand nearer than the nudge, is a mistake in the command line, as no pointer would move; a key is pressed with the focus given to its element as Tab gives it (`ElementHandle.focus`), modifiers joined by `+`, each name one a browser knows (`keysOf()`, puppeteer's keyboard layout); words are typed where the focus goes, the element pressed first and the caret put at the end of what it holds (`caretAtEnd()`), unless the focus is in it already (`typingGoes()`: the element, in it, or an editable element around it, a text part's editor; a step before may have given it, or chosen what the words replace, `Control+a`), ASCII key by key and each other run through an input method, three letters composed and the words committed (`typeWords()`: `Input.imeSetComposition`, then `Input.insertText`, as Microsoft Pinyin sends them and as the editor's scenarios simulate the user's input method), so that a field that waits for `compositionend` before it takes Enter is tried as a Chinese reader uses it; an option of a `<select>` is chosen by its value or its words as a reader picks one (`chooseIn()`: the focus on it, the option selected, then `input` and `change`), one it has not, a disabled one, or what is no `<select>`, a mistake in the command line, its options listed. The page tells what came of each (`record()`, `stepBegins()` forgetting it but the save's values as the next begins): each `doc-component-edit` of the instance and whether the editor took it (the window hears it after the document, where the editor answers), each value `data-ge-save` takes, and an HTML5 drag's `dragstart`, `drop` and `dragend` effect. Each write is waited for until `data-ge-save` has left `saved` and says it again (`saving()`: one that never leaves in 1.5 s wrote nothing; one that stays `held` three looks, `offline` a second with the server answering no more, `stopped`, `reloading`, or `waiting` or `syncing` for 30 s, cuts the trial off), and the file is read as its page shows it (`asShown()`: `shownSource`, as a first save of an edit writes an Agent's plain HTML as the kit's markup, said as that with `--keep`; every `data-block-id` taken out, which a save gives the blocks it writes): each part of the instance with the lines the step's write took out and put in against the file the step before left (`changesOf()`, `linesChanged()` and `partsSaid()` of the check's way back line), then those lines themselves under the Wrote line, `-` and `+` (`changedLines()`, `writtenLines()`: each cut at 160 characters, twelve of a part, its name before them where several were written; the skill test of 2026-11-06 asked for what was written, not its count), a part laid out anew a note (`laidAnew()`), and what stands outside the parts, which no component's write reaches: a change there is someone else's (the user's page, an Agent, with `--keep`), said in the Wrote line as theirs, which stays, and the trial is unverified, to be made again once the file holds still. With `--steps` each step has its `Step N:` line and a `Wrote:` line, `nothing` of one that wrote nothing, which is no failure: a press that opens a field, a selection, words typed before the Enter that writes them. Steps that wrote nothing at all fail, with what came of the last: no `context.edit`, one the page refused (the console's words), one taken that changed nothing, a drag that started and dropped nowhere, the element covered or taking no focus, a field typed in that writes only on a key or as it loses the focus (`nothingSaid()`). A picture of the instance after the last goes to `.gallery/qa/<slug>/try/after.png`, emptied each run, its line asking the Agent to open it, which no run of the skill test of 2026-11-06 did (`OPEN_IT`). Then the undo, as a reader makes it: the focus taken off a field of the instance the steps left it in (`FIELDS`, what `pageUndo` of `installed/page.mjs` leaves a field's keys to, whose Ctrl+Z is its own and which a reader leaves first: `leaveField()`), waited for as a write is, a field that writes as it loses the focus writing then; then Ctrl+Z with the focus where that left it (`undoOnce()`): on a button the page's undo goes on from there, as from a reader's press, and a component that keeps Ctrl+Z from the page there fails. Every `context.edit` the page takes is an undo step of its own, none joined (`history.mjs`), so a Ctrl+Z is pressed for each write, not for each step: `record()` counts the edits the page took (`taken`), and for each step that wrote, the last first, Ctrl+Z is pressed until the instance's parts are as they were before that step, ids aside, at most as many times as the page took edits from that step's start to the start of the next step that wrote (to the undo's for the last, what leaving the field wrote among them), once at least: a press that writes and takes the focus from a field that writes as it loses it wrote twice in one step, and a write that came more than 1.5 s late is counted in the step after and undone with it. Each is waited for as the write was; one that wrote nothing, or parts that still differ once all of the step's were pressed, is a failure saying what still differs; `--keep` makes none. The review of 2026-11-07 found the trial pressing one Ctrl+Z a step, which failed a block whose step wrote twice, and taking the focus off the field right before each Ctrl+Z, which then undid what that wrote in place of the step's write. Last the read back (`readBack()`): what the steps wrote opened in a fresh page of the same browser context (a second scratch copy holding the file as the last step left it, or the document itself with `--keep`), where the instance is to come out of its fallback, to write nothing as it opens (a page would write each time it opened: its file is read before it opens and again once it has come to edit and its instance holds still, the parts to be the same, and `data-ge-save` is heard from then on, so that a write made as the page comes to edit, which `cameToEdit` waits to see saved before the instance is listened to, is found too; the review of 2026-11-07 found one missed so), and to show what the page showed after the last step of what the steps wrote, its words and its fields' values (`wordsOf()`, `wordsDiffer()`). What it shows otherwise is weighed against what the steps wrote (`writtenWords()`: the words a write put in or took out of the lines it changed, read without their tags, JSON keys and entities, and whether it changed how many times they say `true`, `false`, `checked` or `done`; `writtenOtherwise()`: of the words one page shows and the other not, as `wordsIn()` reads them, those the same as a written word or, of words with no spaces between them, as Chinese is written, holding one or held by one, and a box ticked otherwise where a write changed a box's state): a value a component keeps in memory, or writes in a form its mount does not read, shows on the page that wrote it and on no other, and fails, naming those words, the fresh page pictured to `fresh.png`; the rest is what the page showed and never wrote (a filter or a tab chosen, an editor or a menu left open, words typed in a field and not committed), a note saying so, which a fresh page does not keep and need not, and the Read back line says it read back what was written and not the rest. The review of 2026-11-07 found the whole of what the page showed compared, a filter's `<select>` failing a block and the failure telling the Agent to write what it filtered. A mistake in the command line found at a step after others were made (a selector, an option, which only the page can tell) is said after those steps' lines and what the file holds of them (`madeSaid()`): with `--keep`, a Kept line saying `doc.html` holds what they wrote, which stays; on a scratch copy, that it holds nothing of them; exit 2 as any mistake. A trial cut off (a save that stays, a page that loaded itself again since the first gesture, whose Ctrl+Z undoes nothing of it, the browser gone, the page stopping) says what the file held then (`cutOffSaid()`): on a scratch copy what the copy held, which goes, `doc.html` holding nothing of it, unverified; with `--keep` the write kept, or a card held for the reader leaving someone else's write in the part, which stays. What the console of either page says naming the custom block (its script's address, the kit's `[DocKit] <id>`) is a failure at its line of `kit.js`, the rest a note, and a dialog a failure, each once. What a trial says and its exit code are `trialSaid()`'s. Exits as the check does: 1 for a failure, 2 for the command line, said with try's own lines of the usage (`TRY_SYNOPSIS`), 3 when the trial was not seen through.

**What the check and the trial say of controls, examples and layout** (the blind tests of 2026-11-08). The kit holds `[hidden]` over any `display` a block's style gives what stands in an instance (`installed.css`: `@scope ([data-doc-component]) to ([data-doc-slot])`, `:scope [hidden] { display: none !important }`, stopping at a slot, whose blocks keep their own rules), because a ledger's `form.hidden = !context.canEdit()` with `.ledger-form { display: flex }` showed its form in an export, to a reader who could press it to no effect, and nothing said so. `check` says it too: `controlsShown` (`component-look.mjs`) asks the export's page, before any way back (the first of them tells the page it takes edits, for every instance on it), for each instance of a block with a data part of its own (not the settings the board writes) the buttons, fields, selects and textareas of its own markup still shown and enabled, and a note, once, at `kit.js`, names up to three and says that a filter or a tab, which an export keeps, is one to ignore. An example that changes nothing is a `FAIL` at `kit.js` naming the instance it ran on (`template.html`'s empty one, usually), since the check runs `example()` on every instance of both files; `laidAnewOf` leaves a write that lays JSON out as `jsonIn` does, of the old value and of the new, unsaid (a part on several lines closing to one line as its list empties, an entry in it opening to lines of its own as it comes to hold an object; `jsonIn` opens no part that stands on one line, which stays on one line, compact, one that closed to one line too), in the check's way back and the trial's notes alike. `check` and `try` end with a line of their pictures (`unopenedSaid`; qa lists its own as 要看的图): each a `提示：` line, how many pictures and where, none needing to be opened and any open to the Agent: `check` its ten (each document's too, with `--documents`), the two overviews named as the whole of the block; `try` `after.png` and, when it was taken, `fresh.png` (its `Wrote:`, `Undone:` and `Read back:` lines say what the gesture did); `gallery page check` its six, the page at 1280px in the light theme named. Every picture to open was the rule until 2026-11-13, when the user found ten pictures opened each run of a check, again after each fix, where its lines said all there was, and said that what the commands can show stays while what the workflow asks of the Agent is kept to what it needs. The Gesture line of `try --type` says what the field held and that the words went after it, or, where a step before left the focus in it (no press, so the caret is where that step put it: `caretAtItsEnd`), whether they went after it or in at the caret inside it, and `--replace` (`"replace": true` in a step, `stepOf`; `gallery block try` and `gallery page try`) selects all it holds first (`selectAll`, as Ctrl+A does); its note on a fresh page that shows differently what no step wrote says that, and the picture's path, and no more. The `下一步：` line that page check, block check and block try end with gives each picture by its whole path (`unopenedSaid`: the looks of a block in braces, `<folder>/check/{1280,1920,600}-light.png`), where it was a folder, a space and names after it, which a copy of one path took the space with (the blind test of 2026-11-09). `gallery open <name>` of a custom page's name says it is one and gives `gallery page open <name>` (or, for the home page before the server has made it, what it waits for), after the error of a document that is not there; `gallery block new`'s refusal of `--page` beside `--recipe` says, of a recipe that is a page's card already (its places hold `page`), to leave `--page` out, and the usage reads `new <id> ([--script] [--flow] [--data] [--page] | --recipe <name>)`.

**Markdown.** A page export and 复制页面内容 write an instance as its manifest's `markdown` says (`clipboard.read` of the `installed` unit, through `reportMarkdown` in its worker, which reads the catalog for each job): `parts`, the parts in the file's order, a table part as a table, a part edited as text as a paragraph, a slot its blocks each as it is written anywhere (as a column's, unindented), any other its words and pictures; `text`, its words alone, as a whole block's; and so for one of no custom block. A whole block's words are all that it shows (`wordsIn`): a block sets them apart, and so do a `span`, a `label` and a `button` where nothing stands straight in the element around them; next to words of that element's own they are of its line, a unit beside its number. They read the source, so no drawing of a component is in it.

## A custom page

A custom page (自定义页: `<home>/pages/<slug>/`, `src/shared/pages.mjs`; since 2026-11-07) is a page an Agent writes, free HTML (the user's word of 2026-11-09: any HTML, CSS and script of its own; a board of cards, below, is one way, the one the reader rearranges), shown by the workbench beside the documents ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "Custom pages"). It is no document: no editor, no checks of a document's, no export, no full-text search.

- **Its files.** `page.html`, the page as the Agent writes it: its head (`<title>`, its own `<style>`, colors of its own written for both themes, the dark ones under `:root[data-doc-effective-theme="dark"]`), `<main class="gallery-page" data-page-id="<slug>">` holding what it likes of its own (a header) and one or more boards, `<div class="gallery-board" data-board-id>`, each card a `<section class="gallery-card" data-card-id data-x data-y data-w data-h>` around one instance of a custom block, as a document writes it ("A custom block on a page"); and what else the Agent writes, its own scripts, handlers, frames and stylesheets among it (the blocks' scripts and styles are put in as it is shown; its own files in `assets/`, where the server serves a picture by its type, a stylesheet or a script, `.css`, `.js`, `.mjs`, as such, `src/server/media-route.mjs` `PAGE_CODE`, and any other file for saving). A page with no board works as one with: the page service answers it (an add finds no board: `409 board`), its board module starts and edits nothing, the workbench's 添加自定义块 is not offered. `page.json`, `{ title, icon, defaultCards?, hint? }` (`pageMetaOf`), what the sidebar lists it by, and what any page may say it has (no page has anything by its slug): `defaultCards`, Gallery's default cards (`HOME_CARDS`), which 恢复默认卡片 puts back where it misses them (the page service's `restore-defaults`, `pageDefaults`) and its note of new default cards tells of; `hint`, its first-open note on its cards, until the reader closes it (`pageHintShown`). The default home page's says both. `gallery page new <slug> "<title>"` (`src/cli/page.mjs`) writes a new one (`newPageHtml`: the title in `<title>` and one empty board `main`: a page draws no big title of its own, **The board** below), `gallery page list` lists them in the sidebar's order (`listPages`: the head's, kept in the outline file's `head`, [workbench.md](workbench.md) "The sidebar"; a page it names not after it, by `gallery.json`'s `pages.order`, then by title; a page made goes last, `placePageInHead`), and `gallery page open <slug>` gives its address, `/#@<slug>`, on the workspace's server (found or started as `gallery open` does), the page alone (`/pages/<slug>`) as the Agent's. Documents and custom pages share one set of slugs (`slugTaken`, `pageSlugRefused`): `gallery new`, an import and `gallery page new` each refuse the other's, and `pages` is a name no new document takes, as `components` is (`keptName`), though a document of that name made before them stays a document, its folder shared with the pages (no page is called `assets` or `materials`, which such a document keeps there). `layoutOf().customPages` is this folder; the 写作偏好 page's, `.gallery/pages/`, is `layoutOf().preferencesPage`; no key is called `pages` any more (it named `.gallery/pages/` before), so code written for its old meaning fails at once instead of reading the custom pages' folder.
- **Shown at `/pages/<slug>`** (`src/server/routes.mjs`, `src/render/page-html.mjs` `renderPageHtml`), rendered on every request, a page not there passed on (404). The steps of a document's page that take no slug are one function both routes run, `withShownTags()` (`report-html.mjs`; `renderReportHtml` runs it first): the shared tags the source writes and never uses dropped, every shared tag it needs put in (`withKitTags`, with `page`: those every document loads, `ui.js`, and `board.css` and `board.js`), the tags of the custom blocks it holds instances of after them (`withPackageTags`). A document's own steps (its look, its icon, the files of its folder, its page blocks, the descriptions of the other custom blocks) are not a page's. The server adds its live script (`?page=<slug>`: below), the UI's language on its root (`data-ge-language`) and the workbench's session, a module of the editor's under their stamp (`src/editor/page/custom-page.mjs`). The kit's tags under their stamps as a document's are. Its cards go out as `src/shared/page-cards.mjs` `servedPage` reads them: each with the id its first write will give it where it has none (`cardIds`: the lowest `c<n>` no card holds, the same however often the page is read) and its base (`data-ge-base`, what a write to it is held to: below), and by what its block's manifest says (`src/editor/page-service.mjs` `blockOn`) `data-ge-fit="content"`, `data-ge-bare` and `data-ge-state` (`document`: a block whose places lack page; `disabled`: one turned off), the instance of a block that does not run on a page held in a `<template data-ge-held>`, so that nothing of it starts; the live script counts those it holds there as a page's instances, so that a page holding a card of a block turned off, or of documents alone, is shown again once the block is turned on again or its places take page (`components <stamp> <ids>`, its stamp changed). Then the page map (`<script type="application/json" data-ge-page>`: its slug, the server's token, the version it was served at, and each of its cards' blocks with their card, settings, places and data parts), which its board's editing reads, as a document's editor reads its map. None of it is the file's: nothing writes it back. **Its own files**: what the page and its cards show of their own (a picture, a font of its `<style>`) is a file of `assets/` in its folder (`pageFiles().assets`), written `assets/<file>` as a document writes one, which `withPageAssets()` (`page-html.mjs`, by the document's own reader of references, `asset-references.mjs` `referencesWhere` and `addressEdits`: an element's `src`, `href`, `poster`, each `srcset` candidate, a style's `url()`) addresses as `/pages/<slug>/assets/<file>` as the page is served (the page is `/pages/<slug>`, where `assets/` would name `/pages/assets/`), its query and fragment kept, one by a name the route serves none by left as written; the route (`src/server/media-route.mjs` `servePageAsset`) serves a file right in that folder by the document's rule of names (`assetFile`: no folder in it, no `..`), a picture by its type and any other file for saving only (octet-stream, attachment, nosniff), as a document's `asset` and `file` routes do, and nothing else of the folder by its path (`page.html`, `page.json`). A block's write of a part that holds such an address (a data part of HTML with a picture) comes back as the page shows it, which the page service writes back as the folder's (`withOwnAssets`, `src/editor/page-service.mjs`), so that the file never holds the served address; and the parts an edit or a settings answers with (what the page's undo puts on the page and sends back) are addressed as the page shows them, the server handing the service `withPageAssets` as its `shown` (`createRoutes`), so that a Ctrl+Z or a redo of such a write puts back a picture that loads, not `assets/<file>` under `/pages/<slug>`, which names `/pages/assets/<file>`. A page's slug also finds a scratch copy of `gallery page try`'s and `gallery page check`'s, `.gallery/try/try-<8 hex>/` holding a `page.html`, where `pages/` has none of that name (`pageFiles`, as a document's slug finds a `gallery block try` copy): served, written by the page service and kept as any page is, listed nowhere, unwatched (no live channel tells it of its own writes), and gone with its command.
- **Its live channel.** The page listens on `/__gallery/events?page=<slug>`: `page <version>` when `page.html` or `page.json` is written (`pageVersion`, a hash of both, once for a burst of writes; the watcher's change `{ page, name }`, `changeOf`), which a page served at that version does nothing about, and a page whose board edits hands it to the board (`__galleryPageChanged`): a version one of its own writes made changes nothing, any other shows the page again once nothing of the page waits ("Its cards edited"); `assets` (the kit changed), `components <stamp> <ids>` (a custom block it holds an instance of changed) and `settings` as a document's page hears them; `asset <file>=<stamp>` when a file of its own `assets/` is written, made or written again, once it is whole and quiet as a document's (`src/server/asset-news.mjs`, its key `@<slug>`), on which it shows itself again: a page loaded anew asks for its pictures again and is sent the new bytes (an Agent's picture copied over one of the same name; scenario `pagePictureWrittenAgain`). It shows itself again through the workbench when it is in one (`__galleryEditReload`: the session's `reload`, a new page swapped in once drawn), else it reloads; a message it does not know (the workspace's data, to come) changes nothing. A page made or gone, or its `page.json` written, tells the workbench's stream `workspace`, as a document's `doc.json` does; so does `gallery.json` (its `pages.order`). The release server's watcher on Linux watches `pages/` and each page's folder (`src/server/watch.mjs`).
- **The theme** (`ui.js`): on a custom page (`main.gallery-page`, no `.doc-page`) ui.js puts up no display button and keeps nothing; it takes the theme the workbench asks for (`doc-theme-request`) or the system's, tells it as a document's page does (`doc-theme-change`: an instance hears `theme`), and marks the page ready (`data-doc-ready`, which the first paint is held for, as a document's: `base.css`). On every page, a document's too, the root says the theme it shows as `data-doc-effective-theme` (`light` or `dark`), set as ui.js starts and at each change, which a custom page's own colors are written by.
- **A blank page** (the workbench's 新建页面, `POST new-page`: `createPage` with `blank`, its slug `page-<id>`, `newPageSlug`). Its `page.html` is `newPageHtml`'s with an empty `<main class="gallery-page">`, no board: a page no one has written yet (`isBlankPage`: its body that main and nothing else, comments aside), whose page map says `blank` and its `title`, and whose middle shows, of the editor's UI and never the file's (`src/editor/page/board/hint.mjs` `showBlank`, `.ge-page-blank`, the first-open note's look), 这一页还是空的, that an Agent writes a custom page, a page of cards or any page it makes, and a request to copy and say to one (用 Gallery 把「<title>」这个自定义页写出来…, its 复制 as the first-open note's). An Agent's write of `page.html` shows the page again, the note gone.
- **Notes of a page** (`hint.mjs`): on a page whose `page.json` says `hint`, until the reader closes it for the workspace (`POST /__gallery/pages/<slug>/hint`: `closePageHint`, `.gallery/hints.json`'s `pages.<slug>`; its old `home` field read as `pages/home`'s), the first-open note at its top (这一页的卡片可以自己摆: how to move a card, open its menu, add and take one out, and a request to ask an Agent naming the page, `showHint`); on a page whose `page.json` says `defaultCards`, the note of the default cards it was made before and does not hold (有了新的默认卡片, 加到这一页: `showNews`, the page map's `news`, `homeCardsNew`), until they were told of (`tellHomeCards`).
- **The home page is a page as any** (the user's word of 2026-11-09; **The default home page** below says how it is made): no code goes by its slug. It is renamed, trashed and restored as any page, the workbench opens the head's first page and not it by name, `gallery page new home` makes one where none is there (an Agent's, before a server's first start, is then the workspace's and Gallery's is made no more), and what it had of its own is its `page.json`'s, as any page may have it: `{ "title": "首页", "icon": "/icons/lucide/house.svg", "defaultCards": true, "hint": true }` (`HOME_ICON`: Lucide's house in lines, the one the sidebar drew for it before its `page.json` said an icon and the hand of the head's 新建 and 搜索, never a filled icon, the user's word of 2026-11-09; one of Gallery's line icons a page may have, `src/shared/emoji/line-icons.mjs`, `/icons/lucide/<name>.svg`, no kind of a document's icon: the icon route, `iconImage`, draws it in black for the workbench's mask, which paints it in the color of where it stands, and for a browser tab in the tab's gray. A home made while it was MingCute's filled house, `/icons/home-1_lightgray.svg`, a development build's, is given the house in lines once as a start finds it, `adoptOldHome`'s `homeLineIcon` in `.gallery/hints.json`, where its icon is still that one; this over what the item below says of its `page.json`, which was so before). A home made before, while it was special, is given these once as a start finds it (`adoptOldHome`; `.gallery/hints.json`'s `homeAdopted` says it was looked at, so that a page called home made since is left as it is).
- **The board** (`board/`). A board is a grid of square cells, like a phone's home screen: 64px each whatever the window (`CELL`, `src/shared/board-places.mjs`), as a document's reading width is 712px whatever the window, 12 to a row and 16px apart, 944px (`--gallery-board-width` on `.gallery-page`, which `tests/unit/pages.mjs` holds to `CELL` and `GAP`; the page's header and the home page's first-open note are no wider, so that their left edge is the cards'), in the middle of the page, the page 24px in at either side at the least and 40px down from its top (`.gallery-page`; the room a page open alone has its 添加自定义块 in); a card `w` cells wide is `w × 64 + (w − 1) × 16` pixels, and as high by `h`. Folding the workbench's sidebar or widening the window leaves more room on either side and changes no card (the user's word of 2026-11-09: the cell had been `(width − 11 × 16) ÷ 12` of a board up to 1600px wide, so every card grew as the sidebar folded). 64px is the most that keeps twelve columns in the workbench on a 1280px laptop with the sidebar open at its default 270px (a frame 1010px wide, a scrollbar's 15px and the page's 48px leaving 947px), and the cell a 1280px window with the sidebar open had drawn before, which Gallery's own cards were made at; a board is as wide as the room it stands in up to 944px, its columns in its middle (`justify-content: center`), so that a page's own style that widens it moves no card off its grid's middle. `board.js` gives each card its place and size as the CSS reads them, `--gallery-x`, `--gallery-y`, `--gallery-w` and `--gallery-h`, from its `data-x`, `data-y`, `data-w` and `data-h` (whole cells, held to `0 ≤ x`, `1 ≤ w ≤ 12`, `x + w ≤ 12`, `0 ≤ y`, `1 ≤ h ≤ 24`; no number: a 4 × 2 card at 0, 0), and each board its columns as `data-columns` (and as the CSS lays them out, `--gallery-columns`) and its cell's side in pixels as `--gallery-cell`, from the board's own width (`gridFor`) (one ResizeObserver, a MutationObserver for the attributes and cards that come). The rows above a board's first card are not drawn (`--gallery-y` is a card's row less the board's skip, `DocBoard.skipped`; the user's picture of 2026-11-09, the home page's cards a row down under an empty band once its top row's cards were taken out): its first row of cards stands at the board's top, the page's own 40px, wherever the file puts them, the file's rows untouched; while the editing shows a draft no more rows are skipped than as it began, so that a card dragged down from the top row is drawn where it is held, the board closing up once it is dropped, and one held above the first row is drawn at the top, the others under it. A board has two views, each arranged on its own, the same cards on both (the user's word of 2026-11-13: a window too narrow for twelve columns had made the home page's cards impossible to drag, the board flowed and nothing placed there). The wide view, where the room holds twelve columns, draws each card at its `data-x`, `-y`, `-w` and `-h`. The narrow view, where it holds 8 to 11 (the sidebar dragged wide, a window not maximized: `gridFor` of its width, `view: "narrow"`), draws `NARROW` (8) columns of the same 64px cells, 624px, each card at its `data-narrow-x`, `-y`, `-w` and `-h` (`narrowCellsOf`, held to 8 columns), a card that has none at the first free spot by its size (one added in the wide view, or by an Agent's write), and a board none of whose cards has one yet as its wide places one after another (`narrowPlaces`: `flow` by `(y, x)` into 8 columns, each at the first spot at or after where the one before it ends, row by row, that shares no cell, as wide as it is but no wider than the row, so that none goes before one it came after and a room may be left beside a tall one), which nothing writes until the narrow view is first arranged: a drag, a resize or an add there writes every card's narrow place (**The page service**), and the narrow view keeps its own from then on. A room narrower than 8 columns (a phone's, `view: "tiny"`) has as many as it holds, 4 at the least, its cells as large (only under 4, 304px, are they smaller: 4 of them as wide as the board), and draws the narrow view's cards one after another in their order (`flow`), the board in the columns they take (`data-columns`: no empty columns at their right pushing them off the middle), which nothing places or writes (**Its cards edited**). `DocBoard.placeOf(card)` is a card's place in the view its board shows (its wide place, or its narrow one, or where the narrow view puts one of none), what the editing's gestures start from, and `DocBoard.view(board)` which view that is. When a card's size in cells or in pixels changes (a board gone narrow or wide, the window resized, a card's `data-w` or `data-h` written) the window hears `doc-layout-change` once that frame, which the runtime tells every instance as `layout`. A card's frame is one for every card (`--gallery-card-fill`, `--gallery-card-line`, `--gallery-card-radius`, `--gallery-card-pad`, `--gallery-card-shadow`, the kit's card tokens, light and dark: "Design defaults", **Cards**), on the page's ground (`--gallery-page-fill`), its top-right corner the grip's (`--gallery-grip-room`, which a block's first row keeps clear); what does not fit scrolls inside it, and the instance fills it, its own margins and its first and last blocks' outer ones left out. A board with no card yet shows the room of a row of two cells, outlined. A card whose block lays out by its content (`card.fit: "content"`: `data-ge-fit`) is drawn as high as what it holds needs (its instance's height with the card's padding and border, `fitHeight`: `ceil((height + 16) ÷ (cell + 16))`, at least 1, at most 24), measured again as it changes (the ResizeObserver watches its instance too); a board's cards are then laid out by the service's own rule (`settle`, below), the file's places kept and a card under one drawn higher drawn pushed down, never up, so that none shares a cell, while the file says where each card is, which no window's width writes. `DocBoard` gives the board's editing what it reads and draws by: `drawn(card)`, the cells a card is drawn at, and `draft(board, cells)`, the board drawn as a drop would leave it, until the drop or an Escape takes it away: some cards at cells of its own (a dragged card where it would land, a resize), the cards they then meet pushed down under them as the service writes a drop (`pushDown` on the file's cells: `settle` with them kept), and all of it drawn as the file would then be drawn (fit content as high as it is, what is under it lower, the draft's card too), so that a card held over the room a card of fit content takes below its file's cells is shown under that card, where its drop then draws it, never where it would stand only while held. A bare card (`card.bare`: `data-ge-bare`) has no frame of its own, its content at its cells' edges. A custom page shows no big title of its own (the user's word of 2026-11-09: its name is the sidebar's, the tab's and the top bar's): a header's `h1` (`.gallery-page-head > h1`, which a page made before then holds and an Agent may still write) is not drawn, and a header holding nothing else takes no room (`board.css`).
- **Gallery's own blocks for a page** (`src/packs/gallery/`, the pack 「Gallery 风格」; their faces written anew to its mockup on 2026-11-09, version 2.0.0; `tests/unit/shipped-blocks.mjs`), each one of `gallery block add`'s as `card` is, written only with what any block has (`context` and `DocKit.input`, its own parts, the kit's tokens, its face in the card tokens' language, "Cards" above; a JSON part in `template.html` and `example.html` written as `context.editJSON` writes it back, on one line, compact, `{"text":""}`, as `block-script.md` tells an Agent and `--recipe` copies), so that each works on a page through the host, in a document where a document holds one (as `gallery block check` and `try` run them) and in an export, read-only; each passes `gallery block check` with nothing to say, its words its own in both languages, by the kit's rule for which (`data-ge-language`, else the page's `lang`: `languageOf` in each `kit.js`), and its face laid out by its card's cells, not by its pixels: `context.size` (`{ w, h }`, null where no card holds it, a document), read again on `layout`, says which of a few layouts the face's `data-layout` takes (as the mockup draws each size; a card resized to another size takes the nearest), filling its card (`.gallery-card > [data-doc-component=…] { height: 100% }`, the board's frame around it; where no card is, the note frames itself). `gallery/today` (问候与日期; `sizes` 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 1): no part, its face all drawn, a grid of three layouts (`layoutOf`): "full" (4 × 2) a head of the chip (the sun from 5 to 17 in its warm color, the moon after it in the accent's pale), the date and its weekday (600) and the number of the week after it (`weekNumberOf`, ISO 8601's: 第 46 周, Week 46), the greeting 28px under it and the week at the bottom, seven days of 38px, each day's initial over its date, the days gone faint, today's 今 (Today) the one solid square of the accent (`weekOf`, `aria-hidden`, as the date says it); "narrow" (2 wide) a page of a desk calendar, the greeting the head's title, today's day of the month 56px at the bottom, its weekday and month under it (星期四 · 11月); "line" (one cell high) the chip, the greeting (15px) and the date muted on one line; a glow from the card's top left, the sun's warm color by day, the accent's in the evening (the face's `data-sky`), drawn from the card's edges by its face taken out over the card's padding: a greeting by the hour (早上好 5–11, 下午好 12–17, 晚上好 18–4: `partOfDay`), the date and the weekday as a `<time>` in the order its language writes them (11月7日 星期六, Saturday, November 7: `faceOf`'s `line`, pieces a line breaks between and never inside, the weekday's marked, `gallery-today-weekday`), drawn again at each `clock` (the minute moves the greeting, the day the date). `gallery/note` (便签; 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4 listed, and any size from 2 × 2 to 4 × 8, its `min` and `max` beside its sizes freeing it, `any`: the user's word of 2026-11-09 that a note and the recent ones take any height from 2 to 8, their faces filling it, the field whole lines of the card's height): a part `note`, `{ "text" }` in a script of JSON, drawn in a `<textarea>` under its head (the chip, a note, 便签 and how many characters it holds, its blanks not counted, none on a narrow card: the head's `data-layout`), 13.5px (13px narrow) on 26px lines each ruled faintly under its words (a background that scrolls with them), the rest of the card, that is `DocKit.input`'s (written by `context.editJSON` on Ctrl/⌘+Enter or as it is left, never while an input method composes, Esc putting the written words back; read-only while `canEdit()` is false), 写点什么… its placeholder; while the reader types (focused, not read-only) the card is ringed inside its edge in the accent's wash (the note taken out over the card's padding: a block cannot draw outside its card) and a line under the field says the two keys (Ctrl+Enter 保存, Esc 还原; ⌘Enter on a Mac, by the root's `data-doc-platform`); on a card the field is as high as whole lines fit under the head (`round(down, …, 26px)`), over the keys' line too while the reader types (three lines on a 4 × 2 card, two while typing), so that a line it scrolls to is never cut at its top, its top fading over 8px once it is scrolled down (`data-scrolled`), its caret the accent's (the critic of 2026-11-09: the keys' line took 22px, the field was no whole number of lines, and the caret on the last line left half a line at its top). `gallery/static` (静态卡片; places `page` alone, as a document already has a block for whatever only shows and an Agent writes its HTML as it is there, `sizes` 4 × 4 first and the mockup's common ones, 4 × 2, 8 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 1, 2 × 1, `min` 1 × 1 and `max` 12 × 24 beside them so that the corner reaches any whole size, `resize`, `fixed`: its height is its own, never its content's, what does not fit scrolls inside it): no script, one part `content` (`:scope > div`, edited as `none`) holding any HTML, given a light reading look in an `@scope` of the instance by `:where()` rules, which weigh nothing, so that a page's own style or a `style` attribute wins: 13px words on 20px lines; the heading it starts with, where more follows it, the cards' head row (13px, 600, a chip of lines before it, 8px over what follows); a list's items after small dots (a `<small>` in one apart by the space written before it, no margin of its own); a small heading (h4 to h6) a faint label; a paragraph of one link (and a `<small>` after it) a row of 28px, a page's picture before a link of the workspace's and a link's before one of the web, the small word at its end (a domain); a paragraph of `<strong>` alone 15px, and right after the heading it starts with, at the card's foot with what follows it (`margin-top: auto` in the content made a column as high as the card, the float of the grip's corner then the heading's padding, one rule for every size: the mockup's 2 × 2 还差 2 项 / 门禁 · 盲测); a table compact, its heads faint, its rows a hairline apart; a heading alone (a card that names a group of cards) 15px with its small words beside it; a link alone a tile (600, after an arrow); content of one line in the middle of its card's height. It sets no color but the links' (the accent's ink): its words, headings among them, are the color they stand in, inherited (the page's text, or a color the page gives the card or a wrapper of the content), its small words, labels, dots, quotes and lines a share of it (`color-mix()` with `currentColor`), so that light words a page writes on a dark card stay light in every heading (the scenario `staticCardColors`); its first line or two go round a float at the instance's top right as large as the grip's corner (`--gallery-grip-room`, on a card alone, but a card of one line), and its text wraps `pretty`; a picture in it is a file of the page's own `assets/`, written `assets/<file>` (`pages/<slug>/assets/`, served at `/pages/<slug>/assets/<file>`: "Shown at `/pages/<slug>`"), which `gallery page check` says is there, or a `data:` address; an empty one says, by its stylesheet, in either language, that it holds nothing yet, the cards' empty face at its left: 还没有内容 over 跟 Agent 说要在这里放什么. `gallery/countdown` (倒计时; 2 × 2, 4 × 2): `settings` `title` (text) and `date` (date), read from its part `settings` and never written, whole days of the calendar from the reader's today to the date (`countdownOf`, by each day's UTC date, so that a change of the clocks moves nothing), its head the chip (an hourglass) and the day's name alone, one line cut with an ellipsis (倒计时 where it has none), at the bottom the count's label (还有, 已经过去: `faceOf`'s `label`) over the count (44px, 48px on a wide card) and its unit, the date faint under them (2027年2月6日 周六, the weekday short), the face's `data-kind` saying which (on the day itself 就是今天, 24px in the accent's ink, where the count stands); a card four cells wide (`data-layout` "wide") sets the month of the date at its right (`monthOf`: 126px, seven columns Monday first, the weekend fainter, the day itself the one solid square of the accent; a month of six weeks a little closer, so that it fits), or, with no date it can read, the cards' empty face under the head (选一个日子, and where to set one: 在卡片右上角的 设置… 里填日期, 在 设置… 里填日期 on a narrow card); drawn again at the clock's `day`. `gallery/habit` (习惯打卡; 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4), written as the recipe an Agent starts its own from (`gallery block new <id> --recipe habit`): a part `habit`, `{ "name", "days": ["YYYY-MM-DD", …] }` in a script of JSON, the name in a field of `DocKit.input`'s (drawn as the head's title, as wide as its words, a soft fill while typed in; on a narrow card its words over two lines at most, cut with an ellipsis, 12px clear of the card's edge, where the grip's dots end 9px into the content, the field over them unseen, the one a press or Tab reaches, standing in their place while it has the focus: a name of seven characters had been cut to five by the grip's 24px), a button that puts today in `days` or takes it out (`toggled`, each press one `context.editJSON`), the streak back from today, or from yesterday while today is open (`streakOf`), the days in all, all worked out as it draws and none written, laid out by its cells: "wide" (4 × 2) the head (a ringed check's warm chip, the name, 共 41 天 after it), the streak 36px and 天连续 with the button at its right (a pale blue 打卡, a pale green ✓ 已打卡 a press takes back), and this week at the bottom, Monday to Sunday (`weekOf`, `monthOf` of one week), seven circles of 28px 4px in from either side (so that today's second ring, done, stays inside the content on a Monday and a Sunday): a day done the accent with a tick, one missed a soft gray with its initial, today ringed in the accent (今; in English its initial, as "Today" does not fit a circle), filled and ringed twice once done, a day to come an outline; "narrow" (2 × 2) the name alone at the top, a flame and the streak (40px) in the room's middle, the button as wide as the card at the bottom; "tall" (4 × 4) the head, the streak and the button, then 最近五周 and how many of them were done (打了 27 天) over five weeks of circles of 26px (`monthOf`), the days of the streak in the accent, those done before it its pale tint, the others bare, today ringed, the days to come an outline; a habit never checked in (no day at all) says 还没打过卡 (and 今天开始第 1 天 on a wide card) where the streak stands, the chip gray and no count of days (the face's `data-fresh`), until the first check-in; where nothing writes, the button a line saying how today stands; drawn again at the clock's `day`. On a page a note's or a habit's write is taken by the board's editing and written by the page service's `edit` as any block's is (**Its cards edited**: the board says `doc-editable` as it starts, so that `canEdit()` is true), a step of the page's undo; where nothing takes it (an export), `canEdit()` is false and their fields read-only.
- **What a block reads of its card** (the contract with the runtime): the page is `main.gallery-page` (`context.place` `"page"`); an instance's card is its closest `.gallery-card`, `data-card-id` its key; its size `{ w, h, width, height }` is the cells the card is drawn at, `DocBoard.cellsOf(card)` (its `data-w` and `data-h` held to `1 ≤ w ≤ 12` and `1 ≤ h ≤ 24`, the manifest's own range for a card, a value that is no number the default 4 × 2; never the raw attributes, so a card written 30 high is drawn and said 24 high), `w` no more than its board's `data-columns`, `h` the height the board drew it at (`--gallery-h`: a card of fit content as high as its content, `card-size.mjs` `drawnCells`), and the pixels of the card's content box. A page's `data-card-id` and `data-board-id` are `[a-z0-9-]`, one of each in the page; one instance a card.
- **Where the cards go** (`src/shared/board-places.mjs`), the one set of rules the page service, `gallery page add`, the kit's board script and the board's editing place a card by, pure: a card is `{ id, x, y, w, h }` in whole cells; `onBoard` holds one to its board (12 columns, at most 24 high); `gridFor` gives a board's columns, cell and view for its width, `narrowPlaces` the cards of its narrow view (8 columns: each at its narrow place, one of none at the first free spot, a board of none the flow of its wide places) and `flow` cards one after another in fewer columns (**The board**); `settle` lays a board out with none sharing a cell and none moved up (the cards it is told to keep stay, each other card, top to bottom and left to right, goes down until it meets none placed before it, right under the one it met), which leaves a board that shares no cell as it is; `pushDown` puts a card where it is moved, resized or added and the cards it then meets under it, and theirs under them; `landing` is where a card held by a drag lands (the user's word of 2026-11-14: on the home page a card the dragged one only went past flashed down out of its way and back): the whole cells nearest to the card's top-left (as `cellAt` rounds the pointer's), held to the row, where they share no cell with another card; where they cover more than half of exactly one card they meet, or it covers more than half of them, and the two can exchange cleanly (`exchange`), where the exchange puts the held card, with that card's id (`swap`); else where they cover more than half of a card they meet or a card they meet covers more than half of them (more than half of either: the reader puts it there, and the cards it meets give way by `pushDown`); where they only clip the cards they meet, half of neither, the free place nearest the card's top-left among the whole cells around it (the two columns and the two rows it lies between; the top, then the left one of two as near), and where none of those is free, where it landed before (its own place as the drag begins;
