an exchange it made kept while it is one still), so that a card it passes on the way is neither pushed nor put back; the drop sends those cells and the `swap`, which the service writes by `dropped`, and a place asked by its cells (`gallery page card`, 大小 ›, an add, an undo) never comes through it, taken as it is asked by `pushDown`; `exchange` is where two cards go when a drag puts one on the other (the user's word of 2026-11-19, the README's third act: recent edits added beside the to-dos and dragged onto them, the two to change places, where 0.3.0 only ever pushed the card met down): they change sides within the cells they span, side by side (rows in common) each to the other's side, the left one ending where the right one ended and the right one starting where the left one started, each on its own rows (a 4 wide card and an 8 wide one stay packed as they stood, the room between two kept), or where that is not clean both on the lower of their two top rows (recent edits added by the library at the first row's end, rows 0 to 4, beside the to-dos at row 2 under the countdown and the note: both to row 2, whichever is held), one above the other (columns in common) each to the other's end, each in its own columns, or where that is not clean both in the further right of their two first columns, apart on both axes each at the other's top-left corner held to the board (two that share a cell already none); only where that is clean: the two then share no cell, neither shares one with a third card, and the card met goes no higher than it was (of two cards one above the other, the lower dragged onto the upper takes its place and the upper goes down, the board `pushDown` gave, while the upper dragged onto the lower pushes it down as before); else none, the cards met giving way down; `dropped` is the board once a card is dropped, the two exchanged where its `swap` is one `exchange` gives at the cells it is dropped at, else `pushDown`: what the page draws while a card is held (the card it exchanges with drafted where it goes), what it shows at the drop and what the service writes, from the board's places alone, never the pointer's; `shared` the cells two cards share; `freeSpot` is the first place a card of a size takes, the topmost row it fits in and there its leftmost column; `defaultSize` a block's card as it is put on a board (its `card.sizes`' first, else `card.w` and `card.h`, a block that says no height 2 high until the page measures it), `sizeFor` what it may be resized to (the nearest of its sizes, else whole cells between its `min` and `max`), `fitHeight` the cells a card of fit content is drawn, `cellAt` the cell a pointer's pixels are nearest to. Nothing moves a card up: a card pushed down stays where it was pushed, the room another left a room, as on a board things are pinned to.
- **The page service** (`src/editor/page-service.mjs`): the one way a custom page's own gestures reach its `page.html`, `POST /__gallery/pages/<slug>/cards`, checked as every write of the server is (Host, Origin, the token, a JSON body: the edit service's `check`; a command of another workspace refused, `elsewhere`) and run in the queue the edit service's writes run in (its `respond`), one op at a time: `place` (a card at other cells, those it then meets pushed down; a size other than its own only where its block says `card.resize`, held to its `sizes` or its `min` and `max`, or where its block says no height, which the page measured; a drop's with `swap`, the card it exchanges with, written by `dropped` on the file as it is now: the two exchanged where that is still clean there, else the cards met pushed down as by a place of none, which an Agent's write while the card was held can make it; a `swap` that is no string refused, `400 swap`; no command sends one), `arrange` (several cards at cells of their own at once, those they meet pushed down: the page's undo of a move or a resize, which puts back up the cards it pushed); either with `view: "narrow"` acts on the board's narrow view (8 columns, its start the view as the page draws it, `narrowPlaces`) and writes the narrow place (`data-narrow-x`, `-y`, `-w`, `-h`: `withCells`' `narrow`) of every card of the board whose written one is not what the view now has, so that the narrow view arranged once holds a place of its own for each card, the wide places untouched; `add` (a card of a block at the first free spot by its default size, or where it is told, and, where the board's narrow view has places of its own or the add says `view: "narrow"`, at the narrow view's first free spot too, the card on both views; a card put back as remove gave it, its narrow place with it, its id the lowest `c<n>` no card holds, its markup its block's template at the board's indent; a block of documents alone, one turned off or not there refused; one of Gallery's own the workspace has not yet put in first (but one `gallery.json` turns off), `addShipped`, and the catalog taken at once, `catalogTaken`, which tells the pages; or, with `html`, a card as `remove` gave it, put back where it stood under its own id where that is free, nothing of it that would run, `refusedInCopy`), `remove`, `edit` (a block's data part, `part` and `html`, or several, `parts`, written by the editor's rewrite of an instance's data parts, `components/installed/file.mjs`: the part's inside alone, what fits) and `settings` (values of the block's manifest's `settings`, each held to its type, merged into its settings part and written as the kit lays its JSON out, `jsonIn`); `restore-defaults` (a page's whose `page.json` says `defaultCards` alone, `pageDefaults`, `409 defaults` on another: a card of each block of `HOME_CARDS`, `src/shared/pages.mjs`, that no card of it holds, by block id, put in as `add` puts one, in that order, each at the first free spot the ones before it left by its default size, Gallery's own block put into the workspace first; one turned off or not there skipped; no card moved, pushed or rewritten, nothing written where none is missing). Each names its card by its id and is held to the card's base, a hash of its markup as the file writes it (its frame's attributes and its instance, every card with its id): another card changed meanwhile, or the page's head, changes nothing of it; this card changed is refused (`{ refused: "changed" }`, 409), one no longer there too (`{ refused: "gone" }`). A page whose cards have no id gets them with its first write, the ids it was served with. The file is read again right before it is replaced (`writeAtomic`'s `expect`: a write between the two runs the op again on what is there, up to three times) and kept as it was before each write in `.gallery/state/pages/<slug>/`, the last 20 (`KEEP`). Each write is told there too, a JSON line of `writes.jsonl` (`WRITES_FILE`, the last 200, `WRITES`; one that cannot be told is said in the log and takes nothing from the write): `{ at, op, by, cards }`, `at` its time once written, `by` `"agent"` for an op whose body says `agent: true` (`gallery page add` sends it), `"gallery"` for a write Gallery makes of its own accord (`apply`'s `by` option, which no request can set: `makeDefaultHome`'s `restore-defaults` alone, below) and else `"page"`, a reader's gesture, `cards` each card it changed by id with how (`cardsChanged`, `src/shared/page-cards.mjs`: `added`, `removed`, `cells` for another board or a data-x, -y, -w or -h as written, `content` for its markup but its place, the ids a first write fills no change), which `gallery page check` marks the user's own changes and Gallery's by (`pageWritesSince`). An op answers `{ ok: true, version, before, cards: [{ id, board, x, y, w, h, base, block }] }`: the page's version now, the version it was applied to (another than the page knew says someone else wrote it meanwhile), each card as the file says it now, and what the page's undo needs: an add the card's id and markup (`card`, `html`, `measure` where its block says no height, and `pushed`, the ids of the cards it pushed down, which `gallery page add` says), a remove the card it took out (`removed: { id, board, html }`), an edit and a settings each part's inside before and after (`parts`: name → `{ was, now }`, as the page shows them: `shown`, the server's `withPageAssets`), a restore-defaults the cards it put in (`added`: `[{ card, block, html, name }]`, each as an add answers it, `name` its manifest's `{ zh, en }`) and the default blocks it did not (`skipped`: `[{ block, reason, name }]`, `reason` the add's refusal: `disabled` turned off, `missing` not there, `places` its places holding no page, `template` no template.html it can put in (none, or one no menu offers: `templateProblem`), `board` the page holding no board). `POST /__gallery/pages/<slug>/hint`, checked and queued as the cards' ops are, closes that page's first-open note for the workspace (`closePageHint`: `.gallery/hints.json`), after which its page map says no `hint`; with `{ cards: true }`, its note of new default cards (`tellHomeCards`). `GET /__gallery/pages/<slug>/library` (the Host alone, as a workspace GET) lists what a card of the page can be: the blocks whose places hold page and that load (one turned off is none, and the document's menus' switch is no matter here), each with its name, description, pictures (`picture`, `{ light, dark }` by the components' route and the block's stamp, with `en: { light, dark }` where it has an English pair, or null: "The menus' pictures of a block"), default size and whether it has settings, and Gallery's own the workspace has not yet (`installed: false`, its pictures by `GET /__gallery/pages/<slug>/picture?id=<id>&theme=light|dark[&language=en]&v=<mark>`, their bytes as the package ships them, kept by the browser for good under that mark, the file's size and the time it was written: a picture made again under the same version is fetched anew), which `add` puts in, but one `gallery.json` turns off (the catalog says so only of one in the workspace: such a one is neither listed nor put in by an `add`, which refuses it, `disabled`, before anything is copied).
- **Its cards edited** (`src/editor/page/board/`, a module of the editor's that `custom-page.mjs` starts by the page map, on a custom page alone, in a workbench or not; in the editor's look, `look.mjs`: grays for every line and handle, never a bright blue, nothing that snaps hard). The board reads the instance a card holds through `src/editor/components/instances.mjs` (`instanceOf`, `packageOf`, `instanceAround`: the host's own mark, `INSTANCE` of `src/shared/components/installed.mjs`, a `div` or `section` naming its package, which the server's `readPage` and the kit's runtime read too), never by a selector of its own: a card of an `<article data-doc-component="…">` has no instance for the board, as for the server and `gallery page check` (which fails it), and so is offered no 设置… and sends no part of data the service would refuse, where the board once took any element carrying the attribute for one; `tests/unit/engine-names.mjs` holds that no page engine spells `data-doc-component`, and `tests/unit/pages.mjs` that the board and `readPage` tell the same card by the same instance. The grip is the card's one handle, with no ⋯ beside it (a grip and a ⋯ stood outside the card's top-left until the user's word of 2026-11-09): the pointer on a card shows it INSIDE the card at its top-right corner, a 28 × 28 box 6px from the card's top and right edges (left of the scrollbar a card that scrolls has), six dots in the quietest gray that go a shade darker under the pointer, grey behind only while pressed or while the card's menu is open (an icon button). It shows while the pointer is over the card, while its menu is open (the pointer then taking it to no other card) and while the keyboard focus is in the card (`:focus-visible`) or on the grip itself (a `tabindex` while shown; Enter or Space opens the menu), never outside the card. That corner is the grip's: a block keeps its own controls and text out of it (the Agent's references say so). A bare card shows where it stands by a dashed line of the guides' gray. One gesture does both jobs: a press that moves past 4px drags the card, one that moves less, a click, opens the card's menu (and closes it where it stood open as the press began); in a phone's room (fewer than 8 columns) a press that went that far does neither. So that what is dragged and selected in a card is its block's (`DocKit.drag` never moves a card, nor does a drag on a card's body): the card follows the pointer, lifted, with no landing shadow under it (the user's word of 2026-11-09): it is itself drawn at where it would go, where it lands (`landing`, through `DocBoard.draft`: the whole cells nearest to it, unless they only clip the cards they meet, half of neither, when it is the free place around them, else where it was going before, so that a card it only passes never moves: the user's word of 2026-11-14), moved off them by the pointer's offset but standing on them on an axis where that offset is within 14px (`SNAP`, each axis on its own: it snaps to the place as it comes near, and lands where it snaps); put on one card alone that can exchange with it (`exchange`; the user's word of 2026-11-19: a card dragged onto its neighbour, the two change places), it lands where the exchange puts it and that card is drafted where it goes, gliding there on the spring every card making way glides on and back the same way once the pointer goes on; else the cards it is put on, more than half of them or more than half of it, drawn pushed down under it (`dropped`, the service's rule), never up, and no guide line stands where an edge meets another card's; while a card is moved or resized the board shows its lattice as faint grey dots, one where the gaps between four cells cross and as far out on its edges (`.ge-board-dots`, first in the board, under the cards, a radial gradient one cell apart, `light-dark()` gray at about a quarter, faded in, placed by `dotsOn` over all the board draws as it grows), gone with the gesture; the pointer's row is the drawn one plus the rows the board skipped as the drag began (`skipped`), so that a card let go where it was writes nothing; Escape, or the pointer taken away, puts all back, and a drop sends `place` (its `swap` where it exchanges), the board standing at once as the rule has it and then as the service answers, one step of undo for every card it moved (an exchange's two undone and done again together); near the window's top or bottom edge the page scrolls under it, the board growing under a card dragged past its last row: within 64px of the edge (`BAND`; never further, so that a card picked up near the edge sets nothing running), timed by the frames' clock and not by the pointer's events, faster the nearer the edge (the square of how far into the band, the edge itself or past it the most) and never past 720px a second (`FASTEST`, 12px a frame at sixty), gathering that speed over 320ms from nothing (`RAMP`; `motion.mjs` `createEdgeScroll`: the editor's own `edge-scroll.mjs` rule, a third of the depth each frame and nothing to hold it past the edge, ran a page away under a card held at the frame's foot, the user's word of 2026-11-09). Its motion is a phone's home screen's (the user's word of 2026-11-09: the snap and the cards making way had moved in jumps; `motion.mjs`): the card held goes with the pointer at once, and where it comes within the snap of its cells (or leaves it, or the cells it stands on move) it settles there over 180ms on a spring (`createSettle`: from where the pointer carried it, so that its own motion is never delayed and the snap's 14px or less take a few frames, never one); every card a layout change moves (a draft's push as the target changes, the card let go to its cells, or back on Escape, and the others to theirs, 大小 ›, an answer that drew a card elsewhere, an undo's and a redo's `arrange`) glides from where it was seen to its new place in 280ms on the same spring (`glide`: FLIP, a transform from its box before to its box after run to none, its width and height too where its size changed; a damping of 0.8, the crest under 2% past the place, as a CSS `linear()` easing), a card still gliding to where the layout still puts it left to go on and one the change sends elsewhere starting again from where it is seen, so that a target changing under a drag never makes a card jump; the card let go stays over the others while it glides (`data-ge-landing`), its lifted shadow easing in and out (the card's own shadow kept layer for layer and a soft lift, `--ge-board-lift`, added after it, so that only the lift eases: a menu's shadow put in its place made each layer turn into another's, the shadow swelling and shrinking on the way, the user, 2026-11-09; a frameless card takes the lift alone); under `prefers-reduced-motion` nothing glides or settles. A press on the grip, the corner or 添加自定义块 gives the page the keyboard (`window.focus()`), so that in a workbench, whose frame a press kept from moving the focus would not take it, Escape and a menu's keys are the page's. A card whose block says `card.resize` has a corner at its bottom-right, shown with the grip: the card follows the pointer between the least and the most it can snap to (its listed sizes', or its `min` and `max`, never past its row's end; a card its block frees, `any`, any whole size between them), standing at the size a drop gives (`sizeFor`) where its width or height is within 14px of it, one of fit content only getting wider. The card's menu (the grip's click, standing under it, its right edge at the grip's) holds 设置… (a block with settings: a small form of its fields, `settings.mjs`, a text, a number, a select, a date, a switch, a document chosen by its title from the workspace's outline, each its default where the part holds none; a select and a document are a field that opens the editor's own menu card under it (the rows of its options, the chosen one checked, ↑/↓, Enter and Escape from the field, Escape closing the list and not the form), never a native `<select>`, whose popup a browser draws in the system's colours, so that a dark page under a light system showed a white list with pale words, the user's picture of 2026-11-09; Escape, 取消 or a press outside it writes nothing, 保存 or Enter in a field writes what changed by the service's `settings`), 大小 › (its block's listed sizes by width, then height, 2 × 1 before 2 × 2 before 4 × 1, whatever order its manifest lists them in, which puts its default first: the user's word of 2026-11-09 that they read as no order; the one it has checked marked after it, in a card of rows of words alone: no icon column before the numbers and only as wide as they are, the menu's `icons: false` and `compact` of a row that opens a submenu) and 删除. The settings form's buttons are the editor's flat ones (the interface's rule for buttons: no line round either, 取消 with no fill until the pointer is on it, 保存 on a pale blue fill of its own, as 加到首页 of the home page's note is). 添加自定义块: in a workbench (the board is `hosted`: `custom-page.mjs` starts it so when the page has a session) it is the first row of the ··· menu in the top bar while a custom page is open (`Topbar.tsx`, `pageMenuEntries`: a plain row with a plus, no colour, shown once the page answers `add-card`; before 2026-11-09 a button of blue words at the bar's right, and 添加卡片 before that), which sends the session's `add-card` command with the ··· button's place in the frame's coordinates, and the page opens the library there, under the button with its right edge at the button's (in the middle of the window where no place is sent), and adds to the page's FIRST board (a page of several boards is added to from the top bar at its first; `gallery page add --board` is the Agent's way to another); asked again while it stands it closes, and a press elsewhere in the workbench, which takes the frame's focus, closes it as a press on the page does. A page open alone in a tab (no workbench) keeps a button of its own (grey words, no line, no fill until the pointer is on it, then the grey `--ge-hover` and `--ge-press` pressed: no colour) at each board's top right, above it. The library opens in the editor's menu card (its search field filtering it and kept at its top, a block's pictures in a card beside the menu as its row is pointed at or reached by the keys, [editor.md](editor.md) "Its picture in \"/\" and in 添加自定义块"; the card keeps 8px or more from the window's bottom and scrolls inside where its list is longer, the menu's own rule, `pageBoardLibrary` holding it in a window 420px high): a block picked is added by the service at the first free spot by its default size, and the page shown again (the workbench's hidden-frame swap, or a reload on its own), which brings the block's script and style; a block that says no height is then measured once and set as high as its content. A block's own write (`context.edit`, the kit's cancellable `doc-component-edit`) is taken as the document's editor takes it (the change run on a draft of each part, a change that wrote the part on the page refused and put back), its drafts put on the page and sent as `edit`; the board says `doc-editable` as it starts, so that a block's `canEdit()` is true. Each op is a task of the page's (`client.mjs`), the tasks one after another: what the page shows of it is drawn at once, what it sends is built only once the tasks before it are done, their answers taken in (each card's cells and base) and their undo steps pushed, so that a block's two writes in a row, two quick Ctrl+Z on one card, or a Ctrl+Z pressed before a drop is answered (which takes back that drop) each go with the base the answer before gave, none refused as changed. The page's own undo and redo (Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Shift+Z or Ctrl+Y, never in a field or a form; `history.mjs`), a task each, send each step's reverse op: a move's or a resize's `arrange` of every card it moved, an add's `remove` and a remove's `add` of the card as it was (its node put back where the page kept it), an edit's and a settings' `edit` of what the part held; the steps are kept in the tab's `sessionStorage` by the page's slug, so that the page shown again (after an add, or someone else's write) undoes what the one before did, those of a card someone else changed meanwhile forgotten (its base as the page last knew it is not the one it is served with). Each view is edited on its own (the user's word of 2026-11-13): every gesture acts on the view the board shows, from `DocBoard.placeOf` (a card's narrow place, or where the narrow view puts one of none), its maths in that view's columns (8 or 12), and its op says `view: "narrow"` there; a step of undo keeps its view and goes back to it whichever the window shows then (`step.view`); a card added in either view is on both. A phone's room (fewer than 8 columns, the cards flowed) moves nothing: its grip opens the menu and drags nothing (its tooltip says 点击打开菜单 over 窗口宽一些才能拖动), there is no corner and no 大小 ›: a gesture in the flow has no one place in the narrow view that leaves the flow as it was dragged; a wider window gives the board its narrow or its wide view. The grip and the corner are put again on their card whenever the room or a board's columns change (the window resized, the sidebar folded, a card's size told, a board's `data-columns` written: the kit flows the cards, which moves them without changing their size), a card menu open meanwhile following the grip, and closed when the board's columns changed under it (its rows, 大小 ›, read them); the layer they stand in is as wide as the page and clipped there (`overflow-x: clip`), so that a grip left where a wider window put it, hidden or not yet put again, never widens the page (a 320 window scrolled sideways about two runs in ten, `boardNarrowView`). The drag's maths read the board's columns from their own left edge (`geometry`: the board stands them in its middle), and a page alone's 添加自定义块 stands over the last column's right edge. The card of a block of documents alone says 这个块只能放在文档里, one turned off 这个块已停用, in its frame, their menu's 删除 alone. An op refused (a card someone else changed) is said (这张卡片刚被改过，请再操作一次: a toast of the workbench's where the page is in one) and the page shown again; any other failure too. The board answers the workbench session's `add-card` (above), and on the home page two commands more (`custom-page.mjs`): `restore-defaults` (the sidebar's 恢复默认卡片), a task as every op is, the service's `restore-defaults` whose cards are one step of undo (`{ kind: "group", steps }`, a group of adds, undone from its last, done again from its first), the page shown again with their blocks, its answer `{ ok, added, skipped }` what the workbench says; and `undo-restore` (`{ cards }`, the toast's 撤销), that step undone only while it is still the board's last and names those cards (`moved-on` else, the workbench then saying to press Ctrl+Z on the page; `refused` where none of its cards could be taken out, each gone or written since, the step then off the undo as a refused Ctrl+Z leaves it and the workbench saying nothing was undone), both answering `board` from a page whose board did not start. The page is shown again on someone else's write (a `page` message of a version no op of its wrote, or an op's answer applied to a version it did not know) once nothing of it waits: no op unanswered, no drag or resize, no form, no field of a card typed in and not yet left (`DocKit.input` commits as it is left); `prepare-close` waits for the ops sent.
- **`gallery page add <slug> <block> [--board <id>] [--x --y --w --h]`** (`src/cli/page.mjs`): a card of a block on a board (`--board`, else `main`, else the page's first), the block named by its id or, for one of Gallery's own, its name: one the workspace has, whose places hold page (a block of documents alone, one turned off, one not there refused, and one of Gallery's own not yet in told `gallery block add <name>`); the service's `add` (the first free spot by its default size, or the cells given, those it meets pushed down, the lowest `c<n>`, every card's missing id given), through the workspace's running server when there is one (its queue, with its token: an open page shows the card at once, as someone else's write; a block the disk holds and the server's catalog not yet, one just put in by `gallery block add` or written a moment ago, which the server answers `missing` until its catalog watch takes it, or a manifest just edited, which it answers by what it held before, `places` or `settings` (`BEHIND`: refusals the disk read just before did not make), asked again every 250ms, 8s at most; the `places` refusal says what to write to make the block a card too), else the service's own `apply` here on the file, kept first as the service keeps it. Prints the card's id and cells and the cards it pushed down (the answer's `pushed`: none where it went into a free spot, whatever stands under it), and refuses a block `gallery.json` turns off before it would tell `gallery block add` of one of Gallery's own.
- **`gallery page try <slug> <card id> <gesture…> [--keep]`** (`src/cli/page-try.mjs`): `gallery block try` aimed at one card, the trial itself the one function both run (`component-try.mjs` `trial`, with its read back) and what differs between a document and a page its host (`trial`'s `host`: `documentHost`, `pageHost`): the gestures, `--then` and `--steps` as block try reads them (the page command takes them out of its line as block's does, `pairsOf`; a mistake said with page try's lines of the usage, `PAGE_TRY_SYNOPSIS`). The card is found by its `data-card-id` as the page shows it (`withCardIds`: a card with none by the id its first write gives it), its block one the workspace loads, not turned off, with page in its places (else exit 1, as a card holding no instance; a card the page has not exit 2, its cards listed). The trial is on a scratch copy of the page (`scratchCopy` of its folder: `page.html`, `page.json`, `assets/`), its page alone (`/pages/<slug>`, `pageAloneOf`) in a headless browser of its own, on the workspace's server found or started; `--keep` makes it on the page itself. The page has come to edit once the kit says ready and the board said its blocks may write (`doc-editable`); a write is what is sent to the page service, heard on the tab (`pageSaves`: each POST to `…/cards`, answered once its answer's head comes, which the service sends once the file is written; a body nothing reads may never be said to finish loading; its status and, for a refusal, its body say `refused` or `stopped <reason>`), the page held still a moment after the last for one sent right after it. What it wrote is told card by card (`cardChangesOf`: the card's data parts, a part its manifest edits as data, its settings among them, each with the lines it took out and put in, after the ids the service gives the cards that have none), and anything else that changed is a problem, named (`outsideWhat`: a card's place, its board among it, its own frame, its instance outside its data parts, another card, a card in or out, the page's own HTML), never someone else's to keep as a document's trial takes it. The undo is the page's own (the board's Ctrl+Z, `src/editor/page/board/history.mjs`), a press for each write; the read back a fresh page of a second copy; the picture the card in its frame (the host's `framed`). Exits as block try does. `--replace` goes with `--type` as in `gallery block try`, and the trial ends with the `下一步：` line listing the card's picture.
- **`gallery page check <slug>`** (`src/cli/page-check.mjs`): what is wrong with a custom page, each problem `FAIL page.html:<line>: …`, and what changed in it. Its structure (`structureProblems`, read with parse5 as the page service reads it), what still matters on a page that is free HTML (its own scripts, `on…` attributes, frames and stylesheets are none of it: until 2026-11-09 a script was refused): where it has a board or a card, `main.gallery-page` there with its boards in it (a page of its own HTML alone needs none), each board's `data-board-id` and each card's `data-card-id` there, of `CARD_ID_PATTERN` and once in the page, each card a board's child holding one instance right in it, its four cells whole numbers as written (`0 ≤ x`, `1 ≤ w ≤ 12`, `x + w ≤ 12`, `0 ≤ y`, `1 ≤ h ≤ 24`: the raw attributes, never the drawn cells), no two cards of a board sharing a cell, its block in the workspace, loading, not turned off and with page in its places (one of Gallery's own not yet added named with its `gallery block add`), and each reference to a file of its own (`assets/<file>`, or the address it is served at) to one there by a name the route serves, and no frame's but a picture's (the route serves a page's own pictures by their type, its stylesheets and scripts as such, and any other file as bytes for saving, octet-stream with `nosniff` and `attachment`, as a document's `file` route does: an `<iframe>`, `<frame>`, `<object>` or `<embed>` of a file there downloads it; a stylesheet or a script of `assets/` is applied and run, a link, `<a href>`, a font, a video or audio use the bytes, as a document's preview uses its own, and a frame of a picture shows it), a picture, frame, stylesheet or script written relative any other way being one nothing serves (a link is not looked at: it may name another page or a document). Its changes (`pageChanges`, `changesSaid`) against the page as the last check that found nothing saw it (`.gallery/qa/<slug>/checked.html`, written by such a check with the time it read `page.html` as its mtime), else the newest version the page service kept before a write (`.gallery/state/pages/<slug>/`, its time the one in its name), else none (`baselineOf`, its `at`): cards added, taken out, moved (another board a move too, each place then said with its board where the page has more than one), resized, their data parts (the lines), a card's own markup but its place and data, the page's own HTML outside its cards, the ids the service gives and the blanks a card takes with it aside, the cards that did not change counted. Each change the page wrote since that time, a gesture of the user's on it, ends `(the user's, on the page)` (`USERS_MARK`, `markedTheUsers`), a line after them saying to leave those as they are: one the page service's `writes.jsonl` tells `by` the page at the baseline's time or after, of that card and that kind (added, taken out, its cells for a move or a resize, its content for its data or markup), so that a card the user moved or typed in since the Agent last checked is no change the Agent made by mistake, to put back, and the cards `gallery page add` put on (or pushed) are not marked: the review of 2026-11-07 found the list, against a baseline before the user's gestures, naming them as the Agent's own. A card the Agent changed by hand that the page also wrote since is marked all the same: the journal tells cards and kinds, not who wrote which byte. A change the journal tells `by` `"gallery"` and the page did not (the default home page's cards, below) ends `(put there by Gallery)` (`GALLERYS_MARK`, `markedTheUsers`'s `gallery: true`; the user's mark wins where the page wrote that kind of change to the card too), a line after them saying it is Gallery's own doing, neither the user's nor the Agent's, to be left as it is: an Agent's first `gallery page check home` on a new workspace, told against the page as it was before the write that put today and note there (no check has found nothing yet), would else list both as added and mark them the user's, the journal having told that write a reader's. The baseline is left as it is (not the page as `makeDefaultHome` left it): the journal says who wrote, the same way for every write, and what the Agent reads is the plain fact that two cards are on the page it has not checked, said to be Gallery's. A reader's own 恢复默认卡片 from the sidebar is a request, a write by the page, and stays the user's. Then its look (`look`): a scratch copy (nothing a block does as it opens writes `page.html`) on the workspace's server in a headless Chrome or Edge at 1280, 1920 and 600px (twelve columns of 64px the first two, the same cards in the middle of more room; a narrow board's seven or fewer the last) in light and dark (the system's theme, which `ui.js` takes on a page with no workbench), once drawn and still: the page wider than its window, a card drawn past its board's edges, a card whose content needs more than it shows (its `scrollHeight` or `scrollWidth` past its client box: what does not fit scrolls in it), a block that failed (`data-doc-fallback`), a picture that does not load, the console's errors and the page's, a request of the page's own answered 4xx or 5xx (the browser's `/favicon.ico` aside), each problem once with the looks it was found in; a look waits for the page's reads of the workspace (`watchQueries`, `component-look.mjs`: a script every tab of a look is given before the page's own, which counts the page's fetches of the query route while they wait, `globalThis.__galleryQueries`, with the block whose `kit.js` asked read from the stack; `drawn` does not call the page still while one waits, `QUERIES` = 15 s at most, and the same for `gallery block try` and `gallery page try`, whose tabs are given it too): the server's first answer reads every document of the workspace and comes seconds after the page is drawn, which the look took for the page's stillness, so that the blind test of 2026-11-09 found 我的待办, 最近编辑 and 工作区统计 pictured empty under an `OK`; a read still unanswered then is a `note` line (not a `FAIL`: nothing of the card's own is wrong) naming each card of that block, or, where the stack names none, each card of a block whose `needs` hold `workspace`, with the read's kind and the looks, and the `OK` line says how many cards were so pictured, which a reader of only the last line sees; each look pictured whole into `.gallery/qa/<slug>/check/<width>-<theme>.png`, listed, then the pictures not yet opened (`qa.mjs` `unopenedSaid`). Exits 1 for a problem, 3 when the page could not be looked at (no server or browser: its structure checked all the same), else 0, and then keeps the page as the next check's baseline. The baseline is told plainly: `Changes since the last check that found nothing (<time>, local time, <file>)`, or, where none has, `the page as it was before its last write (<time>, local time; used because no check has passed yet)` (it read `no earlier check found nothing to compare with` before 2026-11-09, a double negative both blind Agents of that day and the day before flagged).
  Nothing of its look waits without a bound (the blind test of 2026-11-09, second round: a check that waited 15 minutes at its second picture, the page drawn in its tab and the browser answering another connection; not reproduced in three replays, its cause not found, so every wait is bounded and none is trusted): each step is asked through `answered` (`component-look.mjs`: `ANSWER` 30 s, the page's opening `LOADING` 60 s, its drawing 30 s, whose `limit` the stopped error carries), the look of one width and theme has `LOOKING` = 120 s as a whole (`within`, for what no step's bound holds: a call the browser never answers however it is asked, puppeteer's `page.close()` waiting for the browser to say the tab is gone, with no bound of its own, among them), a tab or a context that does not close is let go after `CLOSING` = 5 s (`closed` in `src/transfer/browser.mjs`, which never fails), `puppeteer.connect` after a browser opened its port is given `STARTING` = 30 s to answer, and the browser's own close kills it. A look that stopped is a `FAIL` of the page (`look`'s `stuck`, `stoppedSaid`: `the browser did not answer within 30 s, waiting until the page was pictured; the look stopped here and the browser was closed, so the looks after it were not made`, with the looks it stopped at), the later looks not made, the browser closed (`running.close()`) before the verdict is printed; what the earlier looks found stays. The look that had begun goes on in the background as a promise nothing waits for, and what it finds after is no one's. `gallery block check` and `try`, `gallery page try` and `gallery qa` ask the same way (`runPage`'s and `trial`'s set-up calls through `answered`, `stuckSaid`, `stoppedTrial`; qa's `limited`), so that a browser that does not answer ends them with the step named, and the tab and the context they close are `closed`.
  A card whose block has a `required` setting (`settings[].required`: weather's city, countdown's date) that its settings part leaves empty, with no default, is a `note` (`emptyRequired` in `page-check.mjs`, said by `emptyRequiredSaid`, printed before the changes, also on the `OK` line as `; and one card asks for a setting no one gave (see "note" above): c8 (city)`): the card draws its ask for one, which a picture of the page says nothing about, and the blind test of 2026-11-09 handed over an empty weather card under an `OK`. `gallery page add` takes `--set <key>=<value>` for each setting (`setsOf`, `settingsGiven` in `page.mjs`: the words after the first `=`, held to the field's type; no JSON, which a Windows PowerShell 5.1 takes the quotes out of), sends them as the add's `settings`, which the page service writes through its own `settings` op in the add's one write (a value its block refuses refuses the add), and says after a card added with none of a required one what to do.
- **The default home page** (首页, `pages/home`: `src/editor/home-page.mjs` `makeDefaultHome`; `pages.homeMade`). A page as `gallery page new` writes one (`newPageHtml`: its `<title>` 首页 and one board `main`, no big title on the page; `page.json` `{ "title": "首页" }`, no icon, the sidebar's house; the title is `homeTitle()`, 首页 on a Chinese system and `Home` on any other, with the page's `lang` zh-CN or en, by the file language of `src/shared/file-language.mjs` at the moment it is made: a home page made before, in the other language, is never renamed), 
and on its board a card of each block of `HOME_CARDS` (`src/shared/pages.mjs`, laid out as the home page of the pack Gallery 风格's mockup: `gallery/today` 问候与日期, `gallery/weather` 天气, `gallery/countdown` 倒计时, `gallery/habit` 习惯打卡, `gallery/stats` 工作区统计, `gallery/todos` 我的待办, `gallery/heatmap` 编辑热力图, `gallery/recent` 最近编辑, `gallery/note` 便签 and `gallery/static` 静态卡片, the four of the workspace reading it, "Gallery's own blocks of the workspace" below; the weather asking for a city, the countdown for a date, the habit for a name and the static card for an Agent's content, each by the cards' empty face), put in by the page service's own `restore-defaults` (above, told in `writes.jsonl` as Gallery's own, `by` `"gallery"`: `makeDefaultHome` calls the service with that `by`, so that `gallery page check` names these cards as put there by Gallery, never the user's gestures nor the Agent's, while a reader's 恢复默认卡片 stays the user's):
each block added to the workspace as `gallery block add` adds it (`components/gallery/<name>/`, its version and fingerprint in `.gallery/blocks.json`), each card of its size on the home (`HOME_SIZES`: the habit 2 × 2, the to-dos 4 × 6, the note 4 × 4; else its block's default, which `restore-defaults` passes as `add`'s `w` and `h`) at the first free spot, the ones before it placed: `c1` today 4 × 2 at 0, 0, `c2` weather 4 × 2 at 4, 0, `c3` countdown 2 × 2 at 8, 0, `c4` habit 2 × 2 at 10, 0, `c5` stats 8 × 2 at 0, 2, `c6` to-dos 4 × 6 at 8, 2, `c7` heatmap 8 × 4 at 0, 4, `c8` recent 4 × 4 at 0, 8, `c9` note 4 × 4 at 4, 8, `c10` static 4 × 4 at 8, 8, twelve by twelve cells with none left empty (`freeSpot` reads the board as a page is read). Made once for a workspace, as a server starts (the dev server's `adoptWorkspace`, `src/server/index.mjs`, before the plugin reads the catalog; the release's `startProductionServer`, `src/server/production.mjs`, before its routes) and by `gallery init` (after a `--from` copy, so that an old document keeps its slug; said in a line), never by another command (`ensureWorkspace`, which each runs, makes none, so that `gallery list` writes nothing): a new workspace has it from its first start, an existing one from the first start of a Gallery that makes it. A workspace only other commands have touched therefore has none yet, which an Agent must not make up for with `gallery page new home` (the server would then find one and only mark it, never putting today and note on it): `gallery page list`, and `gallery page add home` while no `pages/home` is there, say so in their last line (`HOME_NOT_MADE`, `src/cli/page.mjs`: `gallery open --no-browser` starts the server and makes it, then list again), and SKILL.md's Custom pages says the same; they say nothing once the home page was made (`pages.homeMade`, even one the user took away), where a document or the trash holds the slug `home`, or where `GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` keeps a server from making one. `gallery.json`'s `pages.homeMade` is written once it is made (`markHomeMade`: every other key of the file kept, and its layout, as this write is no reader's: the member written into the text as it stands, its line ends, indentation, BOM and what stands on one line kept, `pages` added last laid out as the members beside it or `homeMade` beside `pages.order`; only where the text written would not read as the value meant, a key written twice say, the whole file as `JSON.stringify` lays it out), and from then on nothing makes it again, so that a home page taken away stays away (the workbench then opens the outline's first document, as before there was one); a `pages/home` that is there already, an Agent's, is kept byte for byte and only marked, no block put in for it. A workspace whose `gallery.json` does not read as Gallery's gets none (nothing could say it was made, so it would be made again), nor one whose slug `home` a document or the trash holds (one set of slugs), which is not marked: a later start tries again. Nothing of it stops a start: what it cannot make or write (a `gallery.json` Windows keeps read-only or another program holds, `writeAtomic` giving up with `EPERM`; a file where `pages/` goes) is an answer, `failed` with the step it stopped at (`page`, `cards`, `mark`), never a throw, and the dev server, the release's and `gallery init` say it in a line (`homeFailure`: not made, a later start trying again; made without its cards, which 恢复默认卡片 puts on; made but not marked, a later start trying the mark again) and go on. `GALLERY_NO_DEFAULT_HOME` (set, not to `""`, `"0"` or `"false"`) makes none: the tests' workspaces (the browser harness's server, every unit test through `tests/unit/workspace-env.mjs`), whose scenarios of the home page start the server again without it. The workbench opens it when its address names nothing ([workbench.md](workbench.md) "Custom pages"); its row's menu holds 恢复默认卡片 where another page's holds 移至回收区 (the service's `restore-defaults` through the page's board, one step of its undo). The first time it is opened (the page map's `hint`: the home page, its note not yet closed in the workspace, `homeHintClosed`) a note stands at its top, above the board (`src/editor/page/board/hint.mjs`, editor UI, `data-doc-dev`, never the file's): 这是你的首页, how to move a card (the grip at its top right: drag it), open its menu (a click on the same grip: 大小, 设置, 删除), add one (添加自定义块: in a workbench the first row of the ··· menu at the top bar's right; a page open alone has a button of its own at its board's top right) and take one out (the menu's 删除), and 想要新卡片或新页面，跟 Agent 说 with a sentence to copy (用 Gallery 在首页加一张卡片，记我每天喝了几杯水。别动我摆好的卡片。, naming Gallery so that the Agent takes up its skill) and its 复制 (the clipboard, 已复制, the workbench's toast; the frame given the focus first, `window.focus()`, as the board's grip takes it: its press, kept from moving the focus, leaves it in the workbench, and Chrome refuses a clipboard write to a document without it, "Document is not focused"); its × closes it for the workspace, whichever browser or port shows it next (`POST /__gallery/pages/home/hint`, `.gallery/hints.json`, what Gallery keeps of its own: never `gallery.json`, the workspace's settings, which every watcher hears). In the editor's look: a quiet gray fill 720px wide at most, the sentence on the page's own ground, grays alone, light and dark by the editor's tokens; the board's 添加自定义块 stays clear of it below. A home page made before the workspace's four came (问候与日期 and 便签 alone), or before the pack Gallery 风格's home (the six of 0.2's first home, without the weather, the countdown, the habit and the static card), is never changed by itself: a card put on a board the reader arranged would move what they placed, so it is told once instead. `.gallery/hints.json`'s `homeCards` says which default cards the reader knows of (`homeCardsTold`: those the home was made with, or told of since; `FIRST_HOME_CARDS`, the first two, where it says none, as of a home made before), and while the home page holds no card of one of `HOME_CARDS` it was not told of (`homeCardsNew`), the page map says `news` (`[{ block, name }]`, its name the catalog's or Gallery's own manifest's, `src/server/routes.mjs`), and a second note of the same look stands under the first (or alone at the top: `showNews`): 首页有了新的卡片, their names, and 加到首页, which does what 恢复默认卡片 does (the board's `restoreDefaults`: what is missing alone, in the empty spots, the cards there untouched, one step of the page's undo; the page says what came back), with the line that the home's menu in the sidebar does it later too. Its ×, 加到首页 and any `restore-defaults` that runs (the sidebar's, the board's, the default home made: `tellHomeCards` after the op) write `homeCards` as `HOME_CARDS` (`POST /__gallery/pages/home/hint` with `{ cards: true }`), and nothing says it again; a home whose cards are all there says nothing either.
- **Gallery's own blocks of the workspace** (`src/packs/gallery/todos`, `recent`, `stats`, `heatmap`; their faces Gallery 风格's): four blocks whose places are `page` and whose `needs` hold `workspace`, each reading what it shows through `context.workspace.query` and hearing `data` of it, each with its writing notes, its preview picture, its example, and its layouts by its cells (`context.size`, `data-layout`). 我的待办 (`gallery/todos`, 4 × 4, 4 × 6, 8 × 4) lists the to-dos not done of every document, by document, the one edited last first, each group under its document's icon (the `todos` query gives each to-do its document's `icon`, drawn as the sidebar's), its title and its open count, small and gray, each item a 16px box (its line `--gallery-muted`, 4.4:1), its words (two lines, then an ellipsis, the whole in a tooltip) and, under them (at their right on an 8 × 4 card), the headings it stands under but the document's title, which its group's says; the list scrolls and fades at its foot while there is more under it (`data-more`). Its head says how many are open (8 项; 来自 4 篇文档 on a 4 × 6 card); how many are done is drawn by the room: a 4 × 4 card a line (已完成 19 / 27 and the percent at its end) over a tick an item, done in the accent (one bar past 48); a 4 × 6 card a ring of 64px (its percent in the middle) beside the open count 28px (8 项没完成) and 已完成 19 / 27; an 8 × 4 card a ring of 96px over a key of three rows (已完成, 未完成, 来自文档) in a column of 150px at the left. A click on an item's words opens its document at its block (`workspace.open`), its box (the one box every checkbox in Gallery has, 2026-11: 16px, 5px corners, a 1.25px gray line that darkens while the pointer is on the item, the control blue `#4a81d4` (dark `#4f7fcb`) under a 12px white tick once done; a button of its own, `role=checkbox`, where `workspace.setTodo` is) checks it in its document (the list drawn anew puts the focus back on the control that had it, `data-focus-key`, so that Space and Tab go on from where they were): drawn so at once, written by its ref, kept on the card struck through until the page is shown again, a refusal putting the box back, saying why for a few seconds at the card's foot (这一项刚被改过，已重新读取 and the like) and reading again; once a box is checked on the card its groups keep the order they were drawn in, a document new to the card first; its settings (`settings` part: `document`, one document's alone; `done`, the done ones too) the board's form, read only. With none, the cards' empty face in the middle (还没有待办, where they come from and that [] makes one); all done, a full green ring of 72px with a tick over 待办都完成了. 最近编辑 (`gallery/recent`, 4 × 4, 4 × 2, 4 × 6 listed, and 4 wide any height from 2 to 8, as the note: as many whole rows as the card holds, no room kept for the line of how many more where every document fits; its head the chip, a clock, and how many were edited today, 今天 3 篇) lists the documents edited last (`recent`, 50; and `stats` for how many there are in all) in rows like the sidebar's, 30px, grouped as 今天, 昨天, 本周 and 更早 (`groupOf`: today, the day before, this week from its Monday, earlier), each its icon (from the server's icon route: an emoji, which is Twemoji's picture as the sidebar draws it, or a picture, as a picture, a glyph as a mask in the faint color, none a page; 柔光风格's the same), its title and its time at its end said by its group (`whenOf`: 刚刚 and N 分钟前 within the hour, then the hour, 08:05; yesterday's its hour; this week's its weekday, 周二; earlier the date, 11月7日, the year where it is another), the kit's clock moving them each minute, a click opening it; as many whole rows as the room under the head holds with their groups' titles (`fit`, 26px a title and 30px a row, measured again by its own ResizeObserver on the face), and how many more there are under them (还有 20 篇); a 4 × 2 card (`data-layout` "short") the last three, no groups, their times as a person says them (昨天, 周二). 工作区统计 (`gallery/stats`, 8 × 2, 12 × 2, 4 × 4) has no head: four cells, each its label after a small picture of what it counts, its number (28px; 32px on a 12 × 2 card) and a small chart of its own over a faint line (a card four wide and under four high, as the phone's flow makes of an 8 × 2, which keeps its height and loses its width, is `compact`: two by two, the labels and the numbers alone, 24px, so that all four fit its 144px; its week caption says 刚开始记录 where documents were edited this week but the record of edits has none of it yet, not 这周还没动过): the documents a square each (three rows at most, a square more than one document past that, the rows as even as they can be: 12 in two of 6, never 11 and one alone), those edited this week (`editedThisWeek`) in the accent; the words in all (12.4 万字 from ten thousand) over a line of their total at the end of each of the last 30 days (`trendOf`, from the record of edits, `activity`, its area fading under it, a dot at its end) and what those days added (近 30 天 +6万; never more than the words there are, nor a day below none: the record counts each edit's change and a deleted document takes none of its words out of it); the to-dos done of all (19 / 27) over a bar (70% · 还剩 8 项); the edits this week (the record's, the reader's and the Agent's, counted so since 2026-11-09, where it had been the documents edited) over a bar a day, Monday to Sunday, today's in the accent, the days before it its middle shade, the days to come a stub, against the same days of the week before (比上周同期 +14%; 上周同期没有编辑). In a row a hairline apart, the face taken out over the card's padding at its sides so that each cell has 16px; two by two on a 4 × 4 card. An empty workspace says 0 and what each waits for (新建一篇就从这里开始, 还没有待办, 这周还没动过). 编辑热力图 (`gallery/heatmap`, 8 × 4, 12 × 4, 4 × 4, 12 × 3, 8 × 3, 8 × 2) draws the record of edits as GitHub draws contributions: a column a week, Monday at the top, a square a day in five shades of the accent (their own in dark, the deepest the lightest there) by how the day stands among the days recorded (`levelsOf`: the values at 38%, 68% and 90% of the days with any, so that a busy day does not make every other pale; by the most where fewer than five), today's ringed, a day before the record began a small gray dot (never an empty square), the months' names over the weeks they begin in and 一 三 五 at the left, less to more under them; its geometry its layout's (`layoutOf`, `fitOf`): "full" (four high and wider than four: 8 × 4, 12 × 4) squares of 23px 4px apart, as many weeks as the room holds (14, 26), and at their left a column of 156px of figures (`summaryOf`: 近 14 周, the edits of those weeks 36px, 平均每天 11.6 次, the days with any, the longest run of them and today's, a bar of how the reader's and the Agent's edits split, 你 58% · Agent 42%); "compact" (4 × 4) squares of 21px, ten weeks, under a line of the total (28px) and the days with any, no legend; "strip" (three high: 12 × 3, a year; 8 × 3, the user's word of 2026-11-09, about 33 weeks) squares of 14px, 3px apart, smaller where the card is lower (seven of them and the months' row in its height, 4px at the least), the total and the days with any in the head (过去一年 1,632 次编辑 · 139 天), the legend's end saying since when it is recorded (· 5月4日 开始记录之前); "grid" (two high: 8 × 2, the user's word of 2026-11-09, to see the squares alone) the strip's squares with no head, figures or legend, the months' row the card's first, in the middle of its height, a month's name that would reach into the grip's corner left out (measured against a probe as wide as `--gallery-grip-room`), about 44 weeks; drawn again as its card changes size, `layout` and its own ResizeObserver on the room (the scenario `heatmapCellsFit`). A pointer on a square shows a dark tip over it (11月5日 周四 · 19 次编辑, and 你 12 次 · Agent 7 次), which is its `aria-label` too: over the card, never inside it (the user's word of 2026-11-09: a tip near the card's right edge widened it into a sideways scrollbar), a popover in the page's top layer (fixed to the window where a browser has none), placed in the window by the script, its middle at the square's but held 8px inside the window, under the square where there is no room above, its arrow at the square (`--gallery-heatmap-arrow`), gone as the pointer leaves the squares, the squares are drawn again, the page or a card scrolls, or the instance ends; 柔光风格's heatmap the same. It asks for 53 weeks of days, `activity`'s most, 371, as many as the record keeps and its widest card draws, so that a record older than a year never shows its first days as not recorded yet; a record begun within the week says so (刚开始记录; 从 11月11日 开始记录 and that each day will light a square, its clauses each whole on a line where one fits, so that it breaks after the comma); its one setting, `words` (按字数显示), draws and counts the words a day added instead (按字数, 近 14 周新增, 万字); in an export its figures say the time of the snapshot (截至 11月12日 09:30, the day's name in place of 今天). Their empty states use the cards' one empty face (above): 还没有待办, 还没有文档, every number 0, the record just begun; in an export each says it shows when opened in Gallery (`offline`, as where the server does not answer: the heatmap's over its empty squares, the stats' a dash each), and where the server answered and could not (`failed`) that the workspace could not be read (读不到工作区的待办).
- **柔光风格's page cards** (`src/packs/gallery-soft/`, the pack off at first: `OFF_AT_FIRST`; drawn to its own mockup of 2026-11-09; `tests/unit/shipped-blocks.mjs`, the scenario `softPackCards`): the ten page cards again, each the card of Gallery 风格's of its name, so that a user may switch packs and a page keeps working: its places, needs, settings and data parts that one's (the unit test holds them equal), its `kit.js` that one's logic copied (its pure functions answer alike: the unit test calls both) with a face of its own, its sizes the mockup's (today 4 × 2 and 2 × 2; note 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4, any from 2 × 2 to 4 × 8 as Gallery 风格's; countdown 2 × 2, 4 × 2; habit 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4, the last a calendar of four weeks and the longest streak, `longestOf`, `calendarOf`; weather 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4; todos 4 × 4, 4 × 6, 8 × 4; recent 4 × 4, 4 × 2, 4 × 6, 4 wide any height from 2 to 8 as Gallery 风格's, six high or more labelling today, yesterday, this week and earlier, `sectionOf`; stats 8 × 2, 12 × 2, 4 × 2, 4 × 4; heatmap 8 × 4, 6 × 4, 12 × 4, 8 × 3 (the user's word of 2026-11-09: the figures beside the squares the total and the first two under it, the days with any and today's, no average, most or shares' bar, `data-h="3"`), 8 × 2 (the squares alone, smaller, 3px apart: `data-alone`); static any size, 4 × 2 and the mockup's five listed). Each face is the card's whole room: the block takes its face out over the board's padding (`margin: calc(-1 * var(--gallery-card-pad))`, the static card its content div, `:first-child` to outweigh the board's rule of an instance's first and last child) and gives it the soft frame's padding by the card's size (`sizeOf`: `context.size`, read again on `layout`, the manifest's first size where no card is; the face's `data-w`, `data-h` and `data-pad`: 16px at the sides of a card one cell high, 18px on a 2 × 2 one, 24px on one 3 cells high or more, else 20px; the static card by container queries of its instance, `container-type: size`), less the board's 1px line (`--soft-edge`), painted with the soft card's fill under a faint wash of the accent (the note a sheet of paper, today a glow by the hour); the board's line, radius and shadow stay its own (a pack sets no card frame: the check refuses a selector reaching the card), so that the mockup's ring in its shadow (white at 7.5% in dark, ink at 6.5% in light, with two soft shadows) is drawn as the board's 1px border of `--gallery-card-line` instead, a step brighter round each card in dark and fainter in light: the board's choice for every card of either pack. Its tokens are each block's own (`--soft-*` on the instance, `light-dark()` of the mockup's two palettes), its fonts the mockup's (PingFang SC, Segoe UI for figures). The soft to-dos' rows are the words over the headings they stand under, 28px at least (the section had stood beside them and left the words eleven characters), the keys' ring and a row's fill taking the 6px its list keeps at its sides; the recent rows' list the same. The weather's consent bar (the kit's, over the card) takes its top and sides from the face's padding by `--gallery-consent-top` and `--gallery-consent-pad` on the instance (48px and 20px; 46 and 18 on a 2 × 2 card, 52 and 24 on a tall one, none and 16 on one cell high) and a fill that carries the card's wash on, `--gallery-consent-fill`. The grip's corner: a head keeps its end clear (`--soft-grip-room`, 40px less the face's padding), its quiet meta fading there while the grip shows (`--gallery-grip-shown`); the static card's first element is padded so. Data beyond Gallery 风格's (decided 2026-11-09): the to-dos read `recent` (50) for their documents' icons, the numbers the record of edits (`activity`, 30 days: the words' line and this week's bars), the heatmap the reader's and the Agent's edits of the days drawn (its split bar, its tip); the heatmap's shades by where a day stands among the days of any edit (`levelsOf`), its squares 22px (20px on a 6-wide card) spread to the card's edge (`fitOf`). The cards' empty face (a soft disc of 44px over a line and what to do) is laid out by the room: on a 2 × 2 card a disc of 30px and a line each, cut with an ellipsis, so that the three stand clear of the head; on a card 2 cells high and wider (`data-pad` m) the disc of 36px beside its two lines, which a column would not fit under the head (the mockup squeezed its disc into an oval there); the to-dos' empty face of one document says how to add one too. A share done is said by one rule wherever a soft card says it (`percentDone`, the to-dos' ring and the stats' bar: rounded, 100 only when all are done and 0 only when none is). The habit's week shrinks its discs rather than run past the padding (a long word beside it in English), its never-checked line 还没打过卡 over 从今天开始; where its name ends the head (a 2 × 2 card, or no day in all yet) it reaches as far as the padding, its end masked out under the grip while the grip shows, its 2 × 2 placeholder 起个名字. The static card's link to a page of the workspace (an address with no scheme: `../<slug>/doc.html`, `#…`, `/…`) has a page's picture, any other a link's; on a card 2 cells high and wider its link rows are 26px and 4px under the head, so that three fit above the padding. Where no card is (a document, as `gallery block check` runs it) a face frames itself at its default size.

## A block online

A custom block reaches the web only through Gallery's server, for the hosts its manifest's needs name (`net:<host>`, `net:*.<host>` for the names under it, never the name itself), once the reader allowed it.

- **The route** (`src/server/net-route.mjs`, `POST /__gallery/net`): `{ block, url, as, doc }` (`as` `"json"`, the default, or `"text"`; `doc` the document's slug where the instance is in one), checked as a page's request is (POST, the exact Host, its Origin, a JSON body of at most 16 KB) but for the token, which a page no editor runs on (演示视图, a page alone) does not hold: another site's page cannot send the Origin, nor a JSON body without a preflight no route answers. Answered 200 with `{ ok: true, data, at, cached }` or `{ ok: false, code, … }`, so that a refused card puts no error on the console. In order: a custom block of the catalog in use (`missing` where none loads by that id; one turned off is none), an address it may ask for (`addressOf`, `src/shared/net-policy.mjs`: absolute, http or https, at its scheme's own port, no user or password, its fragment dropped; `url`), on a host its needs name (`hostIn`; `host`), which the reader allowed it (`gallery.json`'s `components.net`; `consent`, with `hosts`, those of its needs not allowed yet), with the workspace's key where the address names `{key}` (`key`, with `host`); then the answer kept of the last `FRESH` (a minute) given again, `cached: true`; else at most `RATE` (20) fetches of that block a minute (`rate` where nothing is kept); then the web, which may refuse `private`, `host`, `url` or `consent` (a redirect), `status` (with `status`: no 2xx, or more than `HOPS` redirects), `type` (an answer that is no JSON or text by its type, or no JSON asked as JSON; one that says no type is read as text), `too-large` (more than `LIMIT`, 512 KB, unpacked: its length said, or read no further) and `offline` (no name resolved, the site or the proxy not reached, a timeout of `TIMEOUT`, 10 s, for all its hops). Any refusal after the address was checked gives the answer kept, whatever its age, where there is one (but a redirect's `consent`): what the card showed before. Only GET; no cookie is sent and none kept (`set-cookie` read by nothing); `accept` asks for JSON or text, `user-agent` says Gallery.
- **The machine reached** (`pinnedHop`, over `src/editor/net-fetch.mjs`'s `fetchPinned`, which reuses its proxy discovery, CONNECT tunnel and unpacking): a hop's host resolved here, once (`dns.lookup`, all its addresses), each address checked (`addressKind`: this computer, private networks, a carrier's 100.64/10, link-local, multicast, the reserved and the broadcast, the documents' and tests' ranges, an IPv4 held in an IPv6 held to that; any one of them makes the host `private`, with the address, but for an https address through a proxy of this computer, below), and the request made to the first address, never to the name again: the name stays for TLS (its certificate held to it) and the Host header, so that a DNS rebinding, a second answer naming this computer, is never asked for. An IP written in the address is checked as one. Through the proxy this computer's browser uses (net-fetch.mjs `proxyFor`: the environment's, Windows' Internet Options, macOS' scutil) an https address is a CONNECT to the checked address and an http one names it in the request, the host's name its Host. `198.18.0.0/15` is what a fake-ip proxy's DNS answers with (Clash and its kind): taken only while the proxy the request goes through is a program of this computer (`proxyHere`), which then reaches what it maps that address to, a machine Gallery cannot check, which is so; with no such proxy it is `private`, and Clash's TUN mode without its system proxy is refused so too. A proxy of this computer may map names to addresses of its own outside that range, which the resolver here then gives (the blind test of 2026-11-09: this machine's answered `api.open-meteo.com` with `127.129.1.73` and `fd00:696e:6974:6578::29:14a`, `api.frankfurter.dev` with `127.129.1.71`, so that every card's fetch was `private` once allowed, Gallery's weather's too): a name whose addresses that are not public are all this computer's (127/8, `::1`) or unique-local ones (fc00::/7: `mapsHere`) is handed by its name to that proxy, while it is a program of this computer and the address is https (`machineOf`: a CONNECT to `<name>:443`, `fetchPinned`'s `address: null`), and the proxy resolves it itself. TLS is then the guard: the certificate is held to the name against the public CAs alone (`tls.rootCertificates`, not `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` nor the system's store, which may hold a CA of a private network's), whatever `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED` says (`rejectUnauthorized` set and `authorized` read: a shell set to `0` for a company's TLS inspection would otherwise turn it off), and the request is handed its socket only once the handshake is done, so nothing of it reaches a machine that fails it. A public CA's certificate proves the name, not that its machine is public: a name answered with a private IPv4 network's address (10/8, 172.16/12, 192.168/16, a carrier's 100.64/10, a link's), alone or beside others, is never handed on, for a company's DNS answers its own names so and their machines may hold such certificates. By a name, what failed (the TLS code, the proxy's status, a reset, a proxy that hangs up on the CONNECT) is the log's line alone, the block told `offline` with one why for all (`<host> resolved to <address>, …, and no site the proxy of this computer reached by its name proved to be it`), so that a block whose author's DNS answers this computer's addresses learns nothing of what answers there. Still `private`: an http address (no TLS to guard it), an IP written in the address, `localhost` and the names under it (`keptHere`), a private network's address, and the same answer with no proxy, a proxy of another machine, or the proxy not running (nothing took the connection, `ECONNREFUSED`; no direct try: the address is this computer's). The 198.18/15 path is kept as it was, the address handed to the proxy, for http too: handing it the name would refuse an http address that works through Clash now. A `private` refusal's `why` (the log's line, and the message of `context.fetch`'s Error) names the host, the address it resolved to (an IPv4 one where there is one) and what kept it (`api.open-meteo.com resolved to 127.129.1.73, an address of this computer or of a private network, and an http address is never handed to a proxy by its name (no TLS to check the site)`), so that a reader's network setup is told from a block's mistake; the proxy's address (`10.20.30.40:7890`, of a proxy of another machine or one not running) is in the log's line alone, never the why a block reads. A proxy that cannot be reached is passed over for the same address direct, checked again for that (a fake-ip address is then `private`); one that answers its CONNECT with another status is `offline`, and so is a TLS handshake that fails (at a checked address its code, `ERR_TLS_CERT_ALTNAME_INVALID`, the why; by a name, above). A redirect (301, 302, 303, 307, 308) is followed here, `HOPS` (5) at most, its address held to all of the above but the rate: another host must be one of the block's needs the reader allowed too (`host`, `consent`), and its Location an address a block may ask for (`redirectOf`: one that does not parse, at another port, with a user in it or not http(s) is `url`, as the block's own address would be, never a throw, so that the answer kept is given). What a refusal says (`why`, and the log's line) names a redirect by its scheme and host alone, the port with them: what follows may hold the key the server wrote in, which a site often sends on in its Location; and any string of a refusal that holds the key (as written or encoded: a type that names it) has `{key}` in its place (`keyless`).
- **What is kept** (`src/shared/net-cache.mjs`): each answer by the address the block asked for (its href, `{key}` as written), in memory (the last 100) and in `.gallery/cache/net/`, a file each (`{ url, at, type, text }`, the last `FILES`, 200), and which document's instances asked for which address and as what (`documents/<slug>.json`, the last 20 of each), what an export embeds. All of it may be thrown away; nothing of it is served or reaches a file of the user's.
- **Keys** (`src/shared/net-keys.mjs`): `.gallery/keys.json`, `{ "<host>": "<key>" }`, kept by `gallery block key <host> <key>` (`--remove`; with no key it says whether there is one; with nothing, the hosts that have one and those the blocks name with none; a key is never printed). A block names where the key goes by `{key}` in its address, its path or its query (`…/latest?apikey={key}`, as written or as a path encodes it, `%7Bkey%7D`); the route writes in the key of the address's host (else of the needs' host it matched), encoded as a part of an address, as it fetches, and refuses `key` where there is none. Not in `gallery.json`: its settings are told to every page and read by Agents, and a workspace is handed on with it; no page, cache, snapshot or export holds a key, all of them knowing the address by its `{key}`. An API that takes its key in a header alone is not reached in 0.2.0.
- **The kit** (`runtime/net.js`): `context.fetch` only for a block whose description's `needs` name a host (`descriptionOf` now carries them), asking whoever serves the page by `doc-net-fetch` (cancelable, `{ ask, answer(promise) }`), which the live script of every page this server serves answers from the route (`netAnswer`, `src/server/routes.mjs`: a document's edited or presented, a custom page's), so that the kit an export carries names no route of the server's; unanswered, it rejects `offline`. A rejection is an `Error` with the route's `code` and its `hosts`, `host` or `status`. Refused for consent, the instance gets a bar the kit draws after it (`.doc-net-consent`, `data-doc-dev` and `contenteditable="false"`: no file, export or copy of the view holds it): 这个块要联网才能显示内容 (in a document) and a button, 允许访问 <host> (or <host> 等 N 个网站, every host in its tooltip, data-doc-tip; the whole its `aria-label`); on a card of a custom page it is a banner (the kit's base.css, in Gallery 风格's look), over the card under its head row on the card's fill (its top the Gallery pack's 16px padding and 20px head, its sides and foot the card's padding, its fill the card's, unless the block says its own as `--gallery-consent-top`, `--gallery-consent-pad` and `--gallery-consent-fill` on its instance, which `fit` carries onto the banner, the instance's sibling, at each layout and once the blocks have drawn after it, or on the card, for a face of other room, such as 柔光风格's of 20px padding and a 28px head), so that the card still says whose it is: the why (13.5px, 600; a reader's line, never the maintainers' 块: the block's own, its manifest's `card.consent`, 允许联网之后显示天气 for the weather, else the kit's 允许联网之后显示内容, Allow internet access to show this) over whose the data is (数据来自 <site>, the site its hosts share, open-meteo.com for the weather's two; else <host> 等 N 个网站; faint) at the left and the button at the right, a pale primary whose words are 允许访问 alone; on a card with under 240px of content (three cells or fewer: `data-doc-net-narrow`, measured as it is drawn and at each `doc-layout-change`) the why (13px, three lines at most) over the button, which is as wide as the card, both at its foot, whose the data is left to the button's tooltip (a row there left the why some 20px, a character a line, and the card scrolled); its press dispatches `doc-net-consent` (`{ block, hosts }`, cancelable): in a workbench the page's session takes it and the workbench opens its consent dialog ([workbench.md](workbench.md), "Components"); a page alone opens the workbench at itself in a tab (`/#@<slug>`, `/#<slug>`). What the page is told of the consents (`doc-settings-changed`'s `net`) starts again (`restart`: destroyed and mounted, whether it ran or had failed) each instance whose block's consents changed, allowed or taken back, and each one refused whose hosts are all allowed now (the first line a page hears is its baseline).
- **An export** (`src/render/net-snapshot.mjs` `withNetSnapshot`, in `inlineReport`, so `gallery export` and the workbench's 导出 alike): a document holding instances of a block that names a host of the net gets, before its `</head>`, `<script type="application/json" data-doc-snapshot data-at="<the export's time>">{ "net": { <url>: { data, at } } }</script>` (`netSnapshot`: the answers its instances asked for last, the most recent first, 20 and 512 KB of their text at most, on a host of those blocks' needs, each `data` as it was asked for; its `<` written `\u003c`), none where nothing is kept. There `context.fetch` reads it (`cached: true`; as `text`, JSON given as its text, and the other way) and rejects `offline` for an address it does not hold, for the block to draw its placeholder. 导出当前视图副本 holds no snapshot, and its instances are `offline`. Other kinds of the snapshot (the workspace's data) are a later phase's.
- **Gallery's weather** (`src/packs/gallery/weather/`, `gallery/weather`): a page's card (sizes 4 × 2, 2 × 2, 4 × 4) of a city's weather from Open-Meteo, no key: `settings` `city` (text) and `unit` (℃ or ℉), its needs `net:api.open-meteo.com` and `net:geocoding-api.open-meteo.com`; the city looked up by name (its first match), the weather fetched at its coordinates to two decimals (now, its wind's direction too, and six days: today's lowest and highest, the five after it ahead), again as its settings change and every 30 minutes. Its face is laid out by its cells (`data-layout`): a head of the chip (a place's pin), the city and when (更新于 09:05; an answer the server kept, given with no network, more than 10 minutes old: a warm sign of no network and when, 昨天 21:40, `faceOf`'s `stale`; on a narrow card, whose head is the city alone, the sign still, its time in its title, so that yesterday's weather never reads as now's); the sky's picture, drawn on a box of 64 in its own colors (the sun's orange, a cloud's gray, the rain's blue, as the instance's custom properties say them light and dark: `PICTURES`, a sun, a moon, a sun or moon over a cloud, two clouds, rain, snow, fog and thunder), beside the temperature (46px, 500) and the sky's words over today's lowest and highest (11° / 18°); at the bottom how it feels, the humidity and the wind (东北风 9 km/h), each after a small picture; a 2 × 2 card the picture and the temperature (40px) at the bottom and one line under them (多云 · 11° / 18°); a 4 × 4 card the next five days at the bottom over a hairline, each a row of its weekday, its sky (22px), its lowest, a bar of its span on the scale of all of them and today's (a gradient cold blue to warm orange, the day's part of it) and its highest. What it says while there is nothing to show is the cards' empty face under the head, a line and what to do: no city (写上一个城市, in 设置… at the card's top right), a city not found, waiting for consent (允许联网之后显示天气, 数据来自 open-meteo.com: the kit's 允许访问 stands over the card), offline (现在连不上网，取不到天气, it fetches again once online), a local address (`private`: 取不到天气 over 网络设置把天气服务指向了本地地址, the reader's network setup and no fault of the card's), failed. 11em high where no card is (a document, as `gallery block check` shows it). It is on the default home page asking for a city; nothing is fetched until the reader names one and allows it.
- **The scenarios' net** (`GALLERY_TEST_NET=<port>`, `localHop`): every hop to the server of this computer at that port, as plain http, the address it stands for in its path (`/https/<host><path>?<query>`), nothing resolved and no proxy, all the rest of the route as it is; the browser scenarios reach no other machine, and the names and addresses are the unit tests' ([testing.md](testing.md)).

## Conventions for kit changes

- Name shared components by behavior. Keep domain terminology in document content or example data rather than component names, selectors, APIs, or reusable file names.
- Keep `skills/gallery/sdk/*.d.ts` in sync with the runtime; add a failing case to `tests/unit/sdk.mjs` for every new option.
- Rendering never rewrites what the preview editor edits as text: a component draws into a host of its own (a chart's host, never the card's title or caption) and does not replace its host element. What it draws is not document content: the editor rebuilds text blocks from the source and ignores the rest.
- A component instance keeps its data with it, in its own markup (declared charts: a JSON script in their host), not in a script elsewhere that refers to it by id; the editor can then delete, copy and move it like any block. Its code (the component, or a `DocCharts.define` type) is defined once.
- A new component's styles go in a file of their own in `src/render/kit/styles/` (named as its editor folder), listed in `styles/index.css`, its script in a file of its own or a module of `tables/` or `charts/`; see "The kit". `tests/browser/kit-diff.mjs` shows what a change reaches on the Component Catalog.
- A new component gets a folder of its own in `src/editor/components/` (its description: its unit kind, whether it is a box or one block, its name; see editor.md, "Components") so the preview editor treats it right, on the page and in the server.
- Update `tests/fixtures/component-catalog/doc.html` with an executable example of each new component or variant, and keep [quick-reference.md](quick-reference.md) and the component's card in [components/](components/) accurate (the Agent's [writing-html.md](../skills/gallery/references/writing-html.md) too, when an Agent writes it as plain HTML, and its [example document](../skills/gallery/references/example-report.html), which holds every block once: `tests/unit/skill-docs.mjs` lists the kinds it must hold). The Component Catalog is a test fixture, in no workspace: edit its file directly (it keeps no block ids). Its sections are grouped by component in four Sheets (正文, 图像, 表格, 状态), each a heading and blocks, with a divider block at the top of all but a Sheet's first; declared charts carry no id.
- Run `npm run kit:check`, and `node src/cli/bin.mjs --workspace <a copy of a workspace> export --all`, before packaging: on a copy, for an export hands every document in first (its file written as its preview shows it, every block given an id), and a workspace in use holds someone's own documents.

## Global appearance default (2026-11-11)

The appearance default is now shared across all storage directories after the user chooses 「设置为全局默认」. shared/workspace-look.mjs first reads userDirectory("data")/appearance.json, including an explicit empty look; legacy gallery.json look is a fallback only before that file exists. Individual document look fields override it as before. Preview, editor, list metadata and offline export share the same resolver. The action no longer removes individual document overrides or rewrites every document.
